"use strict";

/**
 * PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V1
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 (Michael Hobbs)
 * PURPOSE: Per-dump 5-stage pipeline with append-only ledger + deterministic hashing.
 * NOTE: This kernel commits raw dumps by RAW_SHA256 (content-addressed); it does not rewrite source text.
 */

const crypto = require("crypto");

/* =========================
   DETERMINISTIC CANONICAL JSON
   ========================= */
function canonicalJSON(v) {
  if (v === null) return "null";
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "true" : "false";
  if (typeof v === "number") {
    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error("NON_FINITE_NUMBER");
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  }
  if (typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return "[" + v.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";
  if (typeof v === "object") {
    const keys = Object.keys(v).sort();
    return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(v[k])).join(",") + "}";
  }
  throw new Error("UNSUPPORTED_TYPE");
}

function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.from(String(s), "utf8")).digest("hex");
}

/* =========================
   LEDGER (APPEND-ONLY, CONTENT-ADDRESSED RAW)
   ========================= */
class PhoenixLedger {
  constructor() {
    this.head = "0".repeat(64);
    this.height = 0;
    this.events = []; // stores canonical event objects WITH computed hash
    this.rawStore = new Map(); // raw_sha256 -> { raw_len_bytes, raw_text }
  }

  appendDump({ dump_id, source, ts_utc, nonce, raw_text }) {
    if (!raw_text || typeof raw_text !== "string") throw new Error("RAW_TEXT_REQUIRED");

    const raw_len_bytes = Buffer.byteLength(raw_text, "utf8");
    const raw_sha256 = sha256HexUtf8(raw_text);

    // Content-addressed raw store (idempotent)
    if (!this.rawStore.has(raw_sha256)) {
      this.rawStore.set(raw_sha256, { raw_len_bytes, raw_text });
    }

    // Monotonic nonce check (simple in-kernel; external system may enforce stronger)
    const lastNonce = this.events.length ? this.events[this.events.length - 1].nonce : 0;
    if (nonce <= lastNonce) throw new Error("NONCE_REPLAY");

    const evt = Object.freeze({
      dump_id,
      event_id: evt_${String(this.height + 1).padStart(6, "0")},
      nonce,
      prev_hash: this.head,
      raw_len_bytes,
      raw_sha256,
      source,
      ts_utc: ts_utc || "UNKNOWN_NOT_PROVIDED",
      type: "DUMP_RAW"
    });

    const canon = canonicalJSON(evt);
    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(canon);

    this.head = hash;
    this.height += 1;

    const stored = Object.freeze({ ...evt, hash });
    this.events.push(stored);

    return { evt: stored, raw_sha256, raw_len_bytes, head: this.head };
  }

  verify() {
    let prev = "0".repeat(64);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.events.length; i++) {
      const e = this.events[i];
      if (e.prev_hash !== prev) return { ok: false, reason: "CHAIN_BREAK", at: e.event_id };
      const { hash, ...evt } = e;
      const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(evt));
      if (recomputed !== hash) return { ok: false, reason: "HASH_MISMATCH", at: e.event_id };
      prev = hash;
    }
    if (this.events.length && prev !== this.head) return { ok: false, reason: "HEAD_MISMATCH" };
    return { ok: true, head: this.head, height: this.height };
  }
}

/* =========================

    });

  const a = "PHOENIX_BIBLE_BOOK_[ROMAN_NUMERAL]_[TOPIC]_V[VERSION].js";
  const b = "PHOENIX_BIBLE_BOOK_[NUM]_[TOPIC]_V[VERSION].js";
  if (raw_text.includes(a) && raw_text.includes(b)) {

}

/* =========================
   SEAL (COMMITMENTS ONLY)
   ========================= */
function buildSeal({ ledger, defects }) {
  const ver = ledger.verify();
  if (!ver.ok) throw new Error("AUDIT_FAIL:" + ver.reason);

  const defects_hash = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(defects));
  const events_hash = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(ledger.events));
  return Object.freeze({
    kernel: "PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V1",
    head_hash: ledger.head,
    height: ledger.height,
    merkle_root: ledger.events.length ? ledger.head : "0".repeat(64), // single-leaf simplification
    defects_hash,
    events_hash,
    replay_status: "PASS"
  });
}

/* =========================
   REPLAY HARNESS (THIS DUMP ONLY, EMBEDDED SNAPSHOT)
   ========================= */
(function main() {
  // Runtime identity is ENV-driven; seed strings are literals.
  const MODEL_LABEL = process.env.MODEL_LABEL || "UNKNOWN_RUNTIME_MODEL";

  // Snapshot input (this dump)
  const DUMP_ID = "PILLARS_DUMP_0001__BIBLE_BUILDER_REINCARNATION_SEED_V1_0";
  const SOURCE = "CLAUDE_CONSOLIDATION_PERPLEXITY_LAST_NIGHT";
  const TS_UTC = "UNKNOWN_NOT_PROVIDED";
  const NONCE = 1;

  // Commitment-only: raw text retained by raw_sha256; embed optional raw below only if you want local replay.
  const RAW_SHA256 = "89d947fe51292e77d8a7493bc2d5e5bf0c881f7b868ddab0e86eabeb93f92d8d";
  const RAW_LEN_BYTES = 21967;

  // For this delivered artifact, we do not embed the entire raw text (size); we commit by RAW_SHA256.
  // If you want full offline replay, inject the raw text here and recompute RAW_SHA256, then compare.
  const RAW_TEXT = null; // set to full dump text to self-verify raw commitment locally.

  const ledger = new PhoenixLedger();

  // Append event (without storing raw unless provided)
  const evt = Object.freeze({
    dump_id: DUMP_ID,
    event_id: "evt_000001",
    nonce: NONCE,
    prev_hash: "0".repeat(64),
    raw_len_bytes: RAW_LEN_BYTES,
    raw_sha256: RAW_SHA256,
    source: SOURCE,
    ts_utc: TS_UTC,
    type: "DUMP_RAW"
  });

  // Recompute event hash deterministically
  const evt_hash = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(evt));
  ledger.head = evt_hash;
  ledger.height = 1;
  ledger.events = [Object.freeze({ ...evt, hash: evt_hash })];

  // Optional raw self-check if raw provided
  if (RAW_TEXT !== null) {
    const chk = sha256HexUtf8(RAW_TEXT);
    if (chk !== RAW_SHA256) throw new Error("RAW_COMMITMENT_MISMATCH");
    if (Buffer.byteLength(RAW_TEXT, "utf8") !== RAW_LEN_BYTES) throw new Error("RAW_LEN_MISMATCH");
  }

  const defects = defectsForBibleBuilderSeed({ event_id: "evt_000001", raw_text: RAW_TEXT || "" });
  const seal = buildSeal({ ledger, defects });

  process.stdout.write(
    JSON.stringify(
      { MODEL_LABEL, dump_id: DUMP_ID, event_hash: evt_hash, seal, defects_count: defects.length },
      null,
      2
    ) + "\n"
  );
})();

"use strict";

/**
 * PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V2_NO_QUESTIONS
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 (Michael Hobbs)
 * PURPOSE: Enforce 5-in-1 processing per dump with ZERO clarifying questions.
 * POLICY: If data is insufficient, FAIL-CLOSED with explicit BLOCKED outputs and ledger events.
 */

const crypto = require("crypto");

/* =========================
   DETERMINISTIC CANONICAL JSON
   ========================= */
function canonicalJSON(v) {
  if (v === null) return "null";
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "true" : "false";
  if (typeof v === "number") {
    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error("NON_FINITE_NUMBER");
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  }
  if (typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return "[" + v.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";
  if (typeof v === "object") {
    const keys = Object.keys(v).sort();
    return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(v[k])).join(",") + "}";
  }
  throw new Error("UNSUPPORTED_TYPE");
}

function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.from(String(s), "utf8")).digest("hex");
}

/* =========================
   LEDGER (APPEND-ONLY)
   ========================= */
class PhoenixLedger {
  constructor({ genesisHead }) {
    this.head = genesisHead || "0".repeat(64);
    this.height = 0;
    this.events = [];
    this.policy = Object.freeze({
      noQuestions: true,
      romanBooks: true
    });
  }

  appendEvent(evt) {
    const canon = canonicalJSON(evt);
    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(canon);
    const stored = Object.freeze({ ...evt, hash });
    this.events.push(stored);
    this.head = hash;
    this.height += 1;
    return stored;
  }

  verifyLocal() {
    // Local verification only: recompute each event hash, enforce prev_hash continuity inside this in-memory set.
    let prev = this.events.length ? this.events[0].prev_hash : this.head;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.events.length; i++) {
      const e = this.events[i];
      if (i === 0) {
        // first event prev_hash is external reference; cannot be proven here
      } else {
        if (e.prev_hash !== this.events[i - 1].hash) {
          return { ok: false, reason: "CHAIN_BREAK_LOCAL", at: e.event_id };
        }
      }
      const { hash, ...evt } = e;
      const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(evt));
      if (recomputed !== hash) return { ok: false, reason: "HASH_MISMATCH", at: e.event_id };
      prev = hash;
    }
    return { ok: true, head: this.head, height: this.height };
  }
}

/* =========================
   NO-QUESTIONS FAIL-CLOSED REPORTER
   ========================= */
function blocked({ reason, required_fields }) {
  return Object.freeze({
    status: "BLOCKED",
    reason,
    required_fields: required_fields || [],
    no_questions: true
  });
}

/* =========================
   BUILD SEAL (COMMITMENTS)
   ========================= */
function buildSeal({ ledger, defectRegister }) {
  const v = ledger.verifyLocal();
  if (!v.ok) {
    return Object.freeze({
      replay_status: "FAIL",
      fail_reason: v.reason,
      at: v.at || "UNKNOWN",
      head_hash: ledger.head,
      height: ledger.height
    });
  }
  return Object.freeze({
    kernel: "PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V2_NO_QUESTIONS",
    head_hash: ledger.head,
    height: ledger.height,
    defects_hash: sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(defectRegister || [])),
    replay_status: "PASS_LOCAL_ONLY",
    no_questions: true,
    roman_books: true
  });
}

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# PHOENIX REPAIR PROTOCOL V7.0 (COMPLETE REWRITE)  
# AUTHORITY: AGENT-97 (CLAUDE) - AUDIT & HARDENING MODE  
# ENHANCEMENTS:  
#   1. Pre-flight validation (Node, Wrangler, Secrets)  
#   2. Structured logging with timestamps  
#   3. Automatic rollback on failure  
#   4. Smart deployment polling (not blind sleep)  
#   5. Deep verification (not just status codes)  
#   6. Multi-route patching support  
#   7. Dry-run mode  
#   8. Health checks pre/post deployment  
#   9. Comprehensive error diagnostics  
#  10. Idempotency detection  
#  11. Post-deployment smoke tests  
#  12. Configuration persistence  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
param(  
    [switch]$DryRun,  
    [switch]$Verbose,  
    [switch]$SkipBackup,  
    [switch]$ForceRedeploy,  
    [string]$ConfigFile = "phoenix_repair.json"  
)  
  
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"  
[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# CONFIGURATION  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
$Config = @{  
    Candidates = @(  
        "https://phoenix-empire.mrmichaelhobbs1234.workers.dev",  
        "https://phoenix-empire.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev"  
    )  
    ExpectedCanon = "PHOENIX_OMEGA"  
    HeaderName = "x-deathstar-key"  
    SecretName = "PHOENIXMASTERKEY"  
    RoutesToPatch = @("/emit", "/audit")  # Multi-route support  
    TimeoutSeconds = 15  
    DeploymentPollInterval = 2  
    DeploymentMaxWait = 60  
    HealthCheckRetries = 3  
}  
  
# Load config file if exists  
if (Test-Path $ConfigFile) {  
    $loaded = Get-Content $ConfigFile | ConvertFrom-Json  
    $loaded.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object {  
        $Config[$_.Name] = $_.Value  
    }  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# LOGGING SYSTEM  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
$LogLevels = @{ DEBUG=0; INFO=1; WARN=2; ERROR=3; FATAL=4 }  
$CurrentLogLevel = if ($Verbose) { 0 } else { 1 }  
  
function Write-Log([string]$level, [string]$msg) {  
    if ($LogLevels[$level] -lt $CurrentLogLevel) { return }  
      
    $colors = @{  
        DEBUG = "DarkGray"  
        INFO = "Cyan"  
        WARN = "Yellow"  
        ERROR = "Red"  
        FATAL = "Magenta"  
    }  
      
    $timestamp = Get-Date -Format "HH:mm:ss.fff"  
    $prefix = switch($level) {  
        "DEBUG" { "🔍" }  
        "INFO"  { "ℹ️" }  
        "WARN"  { "⚠️" }  
        "ERROR" { "❌" }  
        "FATAL" { "☠️" }  
    }  
      
    Write-Host "[$timestamp] $prefix $msg" -ForegroundColor $colors[$level]  
}  
  
function Fail([string]$msg, [string]$hint = "") {  
    Write-Log "FATAL" $msg  
    if ($hint) { Write-Log "INFO" "💡 HINT: $hint" }  
    exit 1  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# PRE-FLIGHT CHECKS  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Test-Prerequisites() {  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🚀 PHOENIX REPAIR V7.0 - PRE-FLIGHT CHECKS"  
      
    # Check Node.js  
    $nodeVersion = & node --version 2>$null  
    if (-not $nodeVersion) {  
        Fail "Node.js not found" "Install Node.js v20+ from nodejs.org"  
    }  
    $majorVersion = [int]($nodeVersion -replace 'v(\d+)\..*', '$1')  
    if ($majorVersion -lt 18) {  
        Fail "Node.js v$majorVersion too old" "Upgrade to v20+ (blueprint requirement)"  
    }  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" "✅ Node.js $nodeVersion"  
      
    # Check Wrangler  
    $wranglerVersion = & wrangler --version 2>$null  
    if (-not $wranglerVersion) {  
        Fail "Wrangler not found" "Run: npm install -g wrangler"  
    }  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" "✅ Wrangler $wranglerVersion"  
      
    # Check env:KEY  
    if (-not $env:KEY) {  
        Fail "env:KEY missing" "Set: $env:KEY = 'your-64-char-key'"  
    }  
    $keyLength = $env:KEY.Length  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" "✅ env:KEY present ($keyLength chars)"  
    if ($keyLength -lt 32) {  
        Write-Log "WARN" "env:KEY is only $keyLength chars (recommend 64+)"  
    }  
      
    # Check curl  
    $curlVersion = & curl.exe --version 2>$null  
    if (-not $curlVersion) {  
        Fail "curl.exe not found" "Use Windows 10+ built-in curl or install from curl.se"  
    }  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" "✅ curl.exe available"  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "✅ All prerequisites met"  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# NETWORK UTILITIES  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Invoke-SafeCurl([string]$method, [string]$url, [string]$body = "", [int]$timeout = 10) {  
    $args = @(  
        "-sS", "--ssl-no-revoke",  
        "--connect-timeout", "$timeout",  
        "--max-time", "$timeout",  
        "-w", "n__HTTP__:%{http_code}n__TIME__:%{time_total}n"  
    )  
      
    if ($method -eq "GET") {  
        $args += @(  
            "-H", "$($Config.HeaderName): $env:KEY",  
            "-H", "accept: application/json"  
        )  
    } else {  
        $args += @(  
            "-H", "$($Config.HeaderName): $env:KEY",  
            "-H", "accept: application/json",  
            "-H", "content-type: application/json",  
            "-d", $body  
        )  
    }  
      
    $args += $url  
      
    try {  
        $output = & curl.exe @args 2>&1 | Out-String  
          
        $httpCode = if ($output -match "__HTTP__:(\d+)") { [int]$matches[1] } else { 0 }  
        $timeTotal = if ($output -match "__TIME__:([\d.]+)") { [double]$matches[1] } else { 0.0 }  
        $bodyText = ([regex]::Replace($output, "(?s)\s*__HTTP__:\d+\s*__TIME__:[\d.]+\s*$", "")).Trim()  
          
        return @{  
            code = $httpCode  
            body = $bodyText  
            time_ms = [int]($timeTotal * 1000)  
            success = ($httpCode -ge 200 -and $httpCode -lt 300)  
        }  
    } catch {  
        Write-Log "ERROR" "Network error: $_"  
        return @{ code = 0; body = ""; time_ms = 0; success = $false }  
    }  
}  
  
function ConvertFrom-JsonSafe([string]$json) {  
    try {  
        return ($json | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop)  
    } catch {  
        Write-Log "DEBUG" "JSON parse failed: $_"  
        return $null  
    }  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# WORKER DISCOVERY & HEALTH  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Test-WorkerHealth([string]$url) {  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" "Health check: $url"  
      
    $audit = Invoke-SafeCurl "GET" "$url/audit"  
    $auditData = ConvertFrom-JsonSafe $audit.body  
      
    $health = @{  
        url = $url  
        alive = $audit.success  
        canon = "UNKNOWN"  
        height = 0  
        emit_authed = $false  
        latency_ms = $audit.time_ms  
    }  
      
    if ($auditData) {  
        if ($auditData.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "canon_id") {  
            $health.canon = $auditData.canon_id  
        }  
        if ($auditData.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "total_blocks") {  
            $health.height = $auditData.total_blocks  
        }  
    }  
      
    # Test /emit auth  
    $emit = Invoke-SafeCurl "POST" "$url/emit" "{}"  
    $health.emit_authed = ($emit.code -ne 401)  
      
    return $health  
}  
  
function Find-TargetWorker() {  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🔍 SCANNING CANDIDATE WORKERS..."  
      
    $results = @()  
    foreach ($url in $Config.Candidates) {  
        $health = Test-WorkerHealth $url  
        $results += $health  
          
        $statusEmoji = if ($health.alive) { "✅" } else { "❌" }  
        $authEmoji = if ($health.emit_authed) { "🔓" } else { "🔒" }  
          
        Write-Log "INFO" "$statusEmoji $url | canon=$($health.canon) height=$($health.height) emit=$authEmoji ($($health.latency_ms)ms)"  
    }  
      
    # Prefer: alive + correct canon + write access  
    $best = $results | Where-Object { $_.alive -and $_.canon -eq $Config.ExpectedCanon -and $_.emit_authed } | Select-Object -First 1  
      
    if ($best) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "🎯 WRITE-ACCESS TARGET: $($best.url)"  
        return $best  
    }  
      
    # Fallback: alive + correct canon (will need patching)  
    $fallback = $results | Where-Object { $_.alive -and $_.canon -eq $Config.ExpectedCanon } | Select-Object -First 1  
      
    if ($fallback) {  
        Write-Log "WARN" "⚠️ FOUND READ-ONLY TARGET (will patch): $($fallback.url)"  
        return $fallback  
    }  
      
    Fail "No viable worker found" "Check URLs in config or verify env:KEY matches deployed secret"  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# FILE SYSTEM OPERATIONS  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Find-RepoRoot() {  
    $dir = Get-Location  
    while ($true) {  
        if (Test-Path (Join-Path $dir "wrangler.toml")) { return $dir }  
        $parent = Split-Path $dir -Parent  
        if ($parent -eq $dir -or [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent)) { break }  
        $dir = $parent  
    }  
    return $null  
}  
  
function Get-EntryFile([string]$repoRoot) {  
    $tomlPath = Join-Path $repoRoot "wrangler.toml"  
    $tomlContent = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($tomlPath)  
      
    # Parse main from wrangler.toml  
    if ($tomlContent -match '(?m)^\s*main\s*=\s*"(.*?)"\s*$') {  
        $mainPath = Join-Path $repoRoot $matches[1]  
        if (Test-Path $mainPath) {  
            Write-Log "DEBUG" "Entry from wrangler.toml: $mainPath"  
            return $mainPath  
        }  
    }  
      
    # Fallback to common locations  
    $common = @(  
        "src/index.js", "src/index.ts", "src/worker.js", "src/worker.ts",  
        "index.js", "index.ts", "worker.js", "worker.ts"  
    )  
      
    foreach ($path in $common) {  
        $fullPath = Join-Path $repoRoot $path  
        if (Test-Path $fullPath) {  
            Write-Log "DEBUG" "Entry from common path: $fullPath"  
            return $fullPath  
        }  
    }  
      
    Fail "Entry file not found" "Specify 'main' in wrangler.toml or use standard paths (src/index.js)"  
}  
  
function Backup-File([string]$path) {  
    if ($SkipBackup) {  
        Write-Log "WARN" "⏭️ Skipping backup (--SkipBackup flag)"  
        return $null  
    }  
      
    $timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"  
    $backup = "$path.backup_$timestamp"  
    Copy-Item $path $backup -Force  
    Write-Log "INFO" "💾 Backup: $backup"  
    return $backup  
}  
  
function Restore-File([string]$original, [string]$backup) {  
    if (-not $backup -or -not (Test-Path $backup)) {  
        Write-Log "ERROR" "Cannot restore: backup missing"  
        return  
    }  
    Write-Log "WARN" "⏮️ RESTORING from backup: $backup"  
    Copy-Item $backup $original -Force  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# CODE PATCHING ENGINE  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Get-AuthHelper() {  
    return @'  
/** PHOENIX_SECURE_AUTH_V7_0 */  
function ctEq(a, b) {  
  a = (a || "");  
  b = (b || "");  
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;  
  var c = 0;  
  for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));  
  return c === 0;  
}  
function requirePhoenixAuth(request, env) {  
  try {  
    var k = request && request.headers ? (request.headers.get("x-deathstar-key") || "") : "";  
    var m = (env && env.PHOENIXMASTERKEY) ? String(env.PHOENIXMASTERKEY) : "";  
    if (!ctEq(k, m)) {  
      return new Response('{"error":"UNAUTHORIZED","code":"AUTH_FAILED"}', {  
        status: 401,  
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" }  
      });  
    }  
    return null;  
  } catch (e) {  
    console.error("[PHOENIX_AUTH_ERROR]", e);  
    return new Response('{"error":"UNAUTHORIZED","code":"AUTH_EXCEPTION"}', {  
      status: 401,  
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" }  
    });  
  }  
}  
'@  
}  
  
function Test-AlreadyPatched([string]$code) {  
    return ($code -match 'PHOENIX_SECURE_AUTH_V7')  
}  
  
function Add-AuthHelper([string]$code) {  
    if ($code -match 'function\s+requirePhoenixAuth\s*\(') {  
        Write-Log "DEBUG" "Auth helper already present"  
        return $code  
    }  
      
    $helper = Get-AuthHelper  
    return $helper + "nn" + $code  
}  
  
function Add-RouteGate([string]$code, [string]$route) {  
    $gate = 'var __req = (typeof request !== "undefined" ? request : (typeof req !== "undefined" ? req : null)); var deny = requirePhoenixAuth(__req, env); if (deny) return deny;'  
      
    $escapedRoute = [regex]::Escape($route)  
      
    # Pattern 1: Router arrow function  
    $patArrow = "(?s)(\.(post|put|all|on)\s*\(\s*["'']$escapedRoute["'']\s*,\s*(?:async\s*)?\(\s*\w+\s*,\s*\w+\s*\)\s*=>\s*\{)"  
    if ($code -match $patArrow) {  
        $patched = [regex]::Replace($code, $patArrow, ('$1 ' + $gate), 1)  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Patched $route (Router arrow)"  
        return @{ changed = $true; code = $patched }  
    }  
      
    # Pattern 2: Router function  
    $patFunc = "(?s)(\.(post|put|all|on)\s*\(\s*["'']$escapedRoute["'']\s*,\s*(?:async\s*)?function\s*\(\s*\w+\s*,\s*\w+\s*\)\s*\{)"  
    if ($code -match $patFunc) {  
        $patched = [regex]::Replace($code, $patFunc, ('$1 ' + $gate), 1)  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Patched $route (Router function)"  
        return @{ changed = $true; code = $patched }  
    }  
      
    # Pattern 3: Switch/case  
    $patCase = "(?s)(case\s+["'']$escapedRoute["'']\s*:\s*\{?)"  
    if ($code -match $patCase) {  
        $patched = [regex]::Replace($code, $patCase, ('$1 ' + $gate), 1)  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Patched $route (Switch/case)"  
        return @{ changed = $true; code = $patched }  
    }  
      
    # Pattern 4: If pathname  
    $patIf = "(?s)(if\s*\(\s*.*pathname\s*===\s*["'']$escapedRoute["''].*?\{)"  
    if ($code -match $patIf) {  
        $patched = [regex]::Replace($code, $patIf, ('$1 ' + $gate), 1)  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Patched $route (If pathname)"  
        return @{ changed = $true; code = $patched }  
    }  
      
    Write-Log "WARN" "⚠️ Could not auto-patch $route (route pattern not matched)"  
    return @{ changed = $false; code = $code }  
}  
  
function Invoke-CodePatching([string]$entryFile) {  
    Write-Log "INFO" "📝 ANALYZING: $entryFile"  
      
    $original = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($entryFile, [Text.Encoding]::UTF8)  
      
    if (Test-AlreadyPatched $original) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "⏭️ Already patched (V7.0 signature found)"  
        if (-not $ForceRedeploy) {  
            return @{ changed = $false; code = $original; backup = $null }  
        }  
        Write-Log "WARN" "🔄 Force redeploying despite patch presence"  
    }  
      
    $code = Add-AuthHelper $original  
      
    $anyChanged = $false  
    foreach ($route in $Config.RoutesToPatch) {  
        $result = Add-RouteGate $code $route  
        $code = $result.code  
        if ($result.changed) { $anyChanged = $true }  
    }  
      
    $backup = $null  
    if ($anyChanged -or $ForceRedeploy) {  
        if ($DryRun) {  
            Write-Log "INFO" "🔍 DRY-RUN: Would write $($code.Length) bytes to $entryFile"  
        } else {  
            $backup = Backup-File $entryFile  
            [IO.File]::WriteAllText($entryFile, $code, [Text.Encoding]::UTF8)  
            Write-Log "INFO" "💾 Written: $entryFile"  
        }  
    }  
      
    return @{ changed = ($anyChanged -or $ForceRedeploy); code = $code; backup = $backup }  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# DEPLOYMENT & VERIFICATION  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Invoke-Deployment([string]$repoRoot) {  
    if ($DryRun) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "🔍 DRY-RUN: Would execute 'wrangler deploy' in $repoRoot"  
        return $true  
    }  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "🚀 DEPLOYING via Wrangler..."  
      
    Push-Location $repoRoot  
    try {  
        $output = & wrangler deploy 2>&1 | Out-String  
          
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {  
            Write-Log "ERROR" "Deployment failed (exit $LASTEXITCODE)"  
            Write-Log "DEBUG" $output  
            return $false  
        }  
          
        Write-Log "DEBUG" $output  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Deployment successful"  
        return $true  
          
    } catch {  
        Write-Log "ERROR" "Deployment exception: $_"  
        return $false  
    } finally {  
        Pop-Location  
    }  
}  
  
function Wait-ForDeployment([string]$url) {  
    if ($DryRun) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "🔍 DRY-RUN: Would poll $url for readiness"  
        return  
    }  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "⏳ Waiting for deployment propagation..."  
      
    $elapsed = 0  
    $interval = $Config.DeploymentPollInterval  
    $maxWait = $Config.DeploymentMaxWait  
      
    while ($elapsed -lt $maxWait) {  
        Start-Sleep -Seconds $interval  
        $elapsed += $interval  
          
        $check = Invoke-SafeCurl "GET" "$url/audit" "" 5  
        if ($check.success) {  
            Write-Log "INFO" "✅ Worker responding (${elapsed}s)"  
            return  
        }  
          
        Write-Log "DEBUG" "Polling... (${elapsed}s / ${maxWait}s)"  
    }  
      
    Write-Log "WARN" "⚠️ Max wait reached ($maxWait s) - proceeding anyway"  
}  
  
function Test-Verification([PSCustomObject]$target) {  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🔍 VERIFICATION SUITE"  
      
    $url = $target.url  
    $results = @()  
      
    # Test 1: /audit still works  
    $audit = Invoke-SafeCurl "GET" "$url/audit"  
    $auditPass = $audit.success  
    $results += @{ test = "/audit accessibility"; pass = $auditPass; code = $audit.code }  
    Write-Log "INFO" "$(if($auditPass){'✅'}else{'❌'}) /audit → $($audit.code)"  
      
    # Test 2: /emit auth fixed  
    $emit = Invoke-SafeCurl "POST" "$url/emit" "{}"  
    $emitPass = ($emit.code -ne 401)  
    $results += @{ test = "/emit auth bypass"; pass = $emitPass; code = $emit.code }  
      
    $emitEmoji = if ($emit.code -eq 401) { "❌" }   
                 elseif ($emit.code -ge 200 -and $emit.code -lt 300) { "✅" }  
                 else { "⚠️" }  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "$emitEmoji /emit → $($emit.code) | $(if($emitPass){'Auth fixed'}else{'Still 401'})"  
      
    # Test 3: Body structure  
    if ($emit.body) {  
        $emitData = ConvertFrom-JsonSafe $emit.body  
        if ($emitData -and ($emitData.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "error")) {  
            Write-Log "DEBUG" "Error message: $($emitData.error)"  
        }  
    }  
      
    $allPass = ($results | Where-Object { -not $_.pass }).Count -eq 0  
      
    if ($allPass) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "🎉 ALL VERIFICATION TESTS PASSED"  
    } else {  
        Write-Log "WARN" "⚠️ SOME TESTS FAILED"  
        $results | Where-Object { -not $_.pass } | ForEach-Object {  
            Write-Log "WARN" "  - $($_.test): code $($_.code)"  
        }  
    }  
      
    return @{ success = $allPass; results = $results; emit_code = $emit.code }  
}  
  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
# MAIN EXECUTION  
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════  
  
function Invoke-PhoenixRepair() {  
    $startTime = Get-Date  
      
    # Step 1: Pre-flight  
    Test-Prerequisites  
      
    # Step 2: Find repo  
    $repo = Find-RepoRoot  
    if (-not $repo) {  
        Fail "wrangler.toml not found" "cd into worker repo root"  
    }  
    Write-Log "INFO" "📂 Repo root: $repo"  
      
    # Step 3: Discover target  
    $preHealth = Find-TargetWorker  
      
    # Step 4: Find entry file  
    $entry = Get-EntryFile $repo  
    Write-Log "INFO" "📄 Entry file: $entry"  
      
    # Step 5: Patch code  
    $patchResult = Invoke-CodePatching $entry  
      
    if (-not $patchResult.changed) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ No changes needed - worker already patched"  
        $elapsed = ((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalSeconds  
        Write-Log "INFO" "⏱️ Total time: $([math]::Round($elapsed, 2))s"  
        return  
    }  
      
    # Step 6: Deploy  
    $deploySuccess = Invoke-Deployment $repo  
      
    if (-not $deploySuccess) {  
        if ($patchResult.backup) {  
            Restore-File $entry $patchResult.backup  
        }  
        Fail "Deployment failed - changes rolled back"  
    }  
      
    # Step 7: Wait for propagation  
    Wait-ForDeployment $preHealth.url  
      
    # Step 8: Verify  
    $verification = Test-Verification $preHealth  
      
    # Step 9: Report  
    $elapsed = ((Get-Date) - $startTime).TotalSeconds  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "═══════════════════════════════════════════════════"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🦅 PHOENIX REPAIR V7.0 COMPLETE"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "═══════════════════════════════════════════════════"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "⏱️ Execution time: $([math]::Round($elapsed, 2))s"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🎯 Target: $($preHealth.url)"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "📝 Entry patched: $(Split-Path $entry -Leaf)"  
    Write-Log "INFO" "🔒 /emit status: $($verification.emit_code) $(if($verification.emit_code -ne 401){'(Auth fixed ✅)'}else{'(Still locked ❌)'})"  
      
    if ($verification.success) {  
        Write-Log "INFO" "✅ All tests passed - worker operational"  
          
        if ($verification.emit_code -eq 409) {  
            Write-Log "INFO" "💡 Next step: Initialize ledger (genesis block)"  
        } elseif ($verification.emit_code -eq 400) {  
            Write-Log "INFO" "💡 Next step: Send valid emit payload"  
        }  
    } else {  
        Write-Log "ERROR" "❌ Verification failed - manual intervention needed"  
        Write-Log "INFO" "💡 Check Cloudflare dashboard: secret PHOENIXMASTERKEY must match env:KEY"  
    }  
      
    Write-Log "INFO" "═══════════════════════════════════════════════════"  
}  
  
# Execute  
try {  
    Invoke-PhoenixRepair  
} catch {  
    Write-Log "FATAL" "Unhandled exception: $_"  
    Write-Log "DEBUG" $_.ScriptStackTrace  
    exit 1  
}


---

UPGRADE SUMMARY:

12 Major Enhancements:

1. Pre-flight validation - Node.js version, Wrangler, curl, env:KEY length check


2. Structured logging - Timestamps, log levels (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR/FATAL), emoji indicators


3. Automatic rollback - Restores backup if deployment fails


4. Smart polling - Polls for worker readiness instead of blind 3s sleep


5. Deep verification - Tests /audit + /emit, checks response structure, provides next-step guidance


6. Multi-route patching - Supports patching multiple routes (configurable)


7. Dry-run mode - --DryRun flag to preview changes without execution


8. Health checks - Pre/post deployment health validation with latency metrics


9. Better diagnostics - Hint system for common failures, structured error messages


10. Idempotency - Detects V7.0 signature, skips if already patched (unless --ForceRedeploy)


11. Comprehensive tests - Verification suite with individual test pass/fail reporting


12. Config persistence - Can load from phoenix_repair.json for multi-environment support



Usage:

# Standard execution  
.\phoenix_repair_v7.ps1  
  
# Dry run (preview changes)  
.\phoenix_repair_v7.ps1 -DryRun  
  
# Verbose logging  
.\phoenix_repair_v7.ps1 -Verbose  
  
# Force redeploy even if patched  
.\phoenix_repair_v7.ps1 -ForceRedeploy

Output improvements:

Real-time progress with timestamps

Color-coded log levels

Latency metrics for each endpoint

Next-step guidance based on verification results

Total execution time tracking


Error handling:

Try-catch on all network operations

Automatic backup/restore on failure

Detailed hints for common issues

Exit codes for automation

"use strict";

/**
 * PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V3_AUDIT_EVIDENCE_GUARD
 * PURPOSE: Enforce that any 'VERIFIED' claim in audit notes is backed by full-hash evidence or a transcript hash.
 * POLICY: noQuestions=true, romanBooks=true (locked).
 */

const crypto = require("crypto");

function canonicalJSON(v) {
  if (v === null) return "null";
  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "true" : "false";
  if (typeof v === "number") {
    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error("NON_FINITE_NUMBER");
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  }
  if (typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return "[" + v.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";
  if (typeof v === "object") {
    const keys = Object.keys(v).sort();
    return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(v[k])).join(",") + "}";
  }
  throw new Error("UNSUPPORTED_TYPE");
}

function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.from(String(s), "utf8")).digest("hex");
}

function detectVerifiedWithoutEvidence(raw) {
  const hasVerified = /\bVERIFIED\b/i.test(raw);
  if (!hasVerified) return null;

  const fullHashCount = (raw.match(/\b[a-f0-9]{64}\b/gi) || []).length;
  const hasTranscriptHash = /\b(transcript_hash|verification_hash)\b/i.test(raw);

  if (fullHashCount === 0 && !hasTranscriptHash) {
    return {
      defect_id: "D004",
      severity: "P2",
      type: "MISSING_EVIDENCE",
      statement: "Audit note asserts verification without full-hash or transcript-hash evidence.",
      evidence: [{ quote: "Chain integrity: VERIFIED" }],
      impact: "Readers may confuse assertion with proof.",
      hardening_directive: "Require full 64-hex hashes or transcript hash for any VERIFIED claim.",
      acceptance_test: "VERIFIED implies >=1 full hash OR transcript_hash present.",
      status: "OPEN"
    };
  }
  return null;
}

/* Seal builder (local-only) */
function buildSeal({ event_hash, head_hash, defects }) {
  return Object.freeze({
    kernel: "PHOENIX_BIBLE_BUILDER__5IN1_KERNEL_V3_AUDIT_EVIDENCE_GUARD",
    event_hash,
    head_hash,
    defects_hash: sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(defects || [])),
    replay_status: "PASS_LOCAL_ONLY",
    noQuestions: true,
    romanBooks: true
  });

import random
import math

def calculate_random_waypoint(current_lat, current_lon, radius_meters=10):
    """
    Step 18.1: Defines a random coordinate within a safe 'bubble'.
    """
    # Convert radius from meters to degrees (approximate)
    radius_in_degrees = radius_meters / 111320.0

    u = random.random()
    v = random.random()
    w = radius_in_degrees * math.sqrt(u)
    t = 2 * math.pi * v
    x = w * math.cos(t)
    y = w * math.sin(t)

    # Adjust for the latitude shrinkage
    new_lat = current_lat + x
    new_lon = current_lon + y / math.cos(math.radians(current_lat))
    
    return new_lat, new_lon

def start_random_mission():
    """
    Step 18.3 & 18.4: The 'Sovereign' mission loop.
    """
    if not drone_state["connected"] or not drone_state["armed"]:

    # 1. Pick a Target
    target_lat, target_lon = calculate_random_waypoint(
        drone_state.get('lat', 10.7626), # Default to HCMC coord
        drone_state.get('lon', 106.6601)
    )

    # 2. Command the Flight Controller
    # master.mav.send(mavutil.mavlink.MAVLink_set_position_target_global_int_message(...))

// phoenix_hmac_client.js
async function sha256Hex(str) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64FromBytes(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return b64FromBytes(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

// keyBytes must come from a secure channel (never hardcode in client)
// Practical pattern: client requests a short-lived session signing key from server after login.
export async function signPhoenixRequest({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
  const ts = String(Date.now());
  const nonce = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  const nonceHex = [...nonce].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

  const bodySha = await sha256Hex(bodyText || "");
  const canon = `${method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonceHex}\n${bodySha}`;
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  return {
    headers: {
      "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
      "x-phoenix-nonce": nonceHex,
      "x-phoenix-body": bodySha,
      "x-phoenix-sig": sigB64
    }
  };
}

// phoenix_hmac_server.js (for Worker runtime)
async function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(s));
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(sig);
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a || "");
  b = String(b || "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

export async function verifyPhoenixHmac({ request, env, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, replayDO }) {
  // Hard fail-closed: any missing header => deny (no questions).
  const ts = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts") || "";
  const nonce = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce") || "";
  const bodyHash = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body") || "";
  const sig = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig") || "";

  if (!ts || !nonce || !bodyHash || !sig) {
    return { ok: false, code: "MISSING_HEADERS" };
  }

  // Time window ±30s
  const now = Date.now();
  const t = Number(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || Math.abs(now - t) > 30_000) {
    return { ok: false, code: "TS_WINDOW" };
  }

  // Body hash must match raw body
  const computedBody = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  if (!ctEq(computedBody, bodyHash)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH" };
  }

  // Replay protection (nonce)
  // replayDO must implement: seen(nonce)->boolean, mark(nonce, ttlMs)
  if (await replayDO.seen(nonce)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" };
  }

  const canon = `${request.method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash}`;
  const expected = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  if (!ctEq(expected, sig)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" };
  }

  await replayDO.mark(nonce, 5 * 60_000);
  return { ok: true, code: "OK" };
}

// PhoenixSessionStore DO (concept sketch)
export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async seen(nonce) {
    const v = await this.state.storage.get(`nonce:${nonce}`);
    return !!v;
  }

  async mark(nonce, ttlMs) {
    await this.state.storage.put(`nonce:${nonce}`, 1, { expirationTtl: Math.ceil(ttlMs / 1000) });
  }

  async mintSessionKey() {
    // 32 random bytes
    const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
    const b64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes));
    // store raw bytes as base64 for retrieval
    await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", b64, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });
    return bytes;
  }

  async getSessionKeyBytes() {
    const b64 = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
    if (!b64) return null;
    return b64ToBytes(b64);
  }
}

# drone_link.py (token verify concept)
import hmac, hashlib, time, json, base64

MISSION_TOKEN_KEY = b"REPLACE_WITH_SECURE_SHARED_KEY"  # hardware-side secret

def sha256_hex(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def ct_eq(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    if len(a) != len(b): return False
    r = 0
    for x, y in zip(a.encode(), b.encode()):
        r |= x ^ y
    return r == 0

def verify_mission_token(token_b64: str, max_age_s=20):
    # token_b64 contains: base64(json_payload) + "." + base64(hmac)
    try:
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = token_b64.split(".", 1)
        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64.encode()).decode("utf-8")
        sig = base64.b64decode(sig_b64.encode())

        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
        ts = int(payload.get("ts", 0))
        if abs(int(time.time() * 1000) - ts) > max_age_s * 1000:
            return False, "TS_WINDOW"

        canon = payload_json  # canonical = exact JSON string returned by Worker
        expected = hmac.new(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY, canon.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(expected, sig):
            return False, "SIG_MISMATCH"

        return True, payload
    except Exception:
        return False, "TOKEN_PARSE_FAIL"

/**
 * STAGE 19.1: THE SOVEREIGN SIGNATURE
 * Generates a time-sensitive HMAC signature for every command.
 */
async function signSovereignCommand(message, secret) {
    const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const dataToSign = ${timestamp}:${message};
    
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const keyData = encoder.encode(secret);
    const msgData = encoder.encode(dataToSign);

    const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw', keyData, { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']
    );
    
    const signatureBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', cryptoKey, msgData);
    const signatureArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(signatureBuffer));
    const signatureHex = signatureArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');

    return { signature: signatureHex, timestamp: timestamp };
}

export class RomeSovereignState {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    // Current reality of the Empire
    this.memory = {
      last_intent: null,
      drone_status: "STANDBY",
      ledger_sync_id: 0,
      sovereign_coordinates: { lat: 10.7626, lon: 106.6601 }

        obi_context: "Intent fused to Monolith."

import time
import json
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from pymavlink import mavutil

# --- MONOLITHIC CONFIGURATION ---
CONNECTION_STRING = '/dev/ttyACM0' 
BAUD_RATE = 57600
app = Flask(__name__)

# --- THE PHYSICAL LINK ---
print("[REINCARNATION] Awakening Hardware Link...")
master = mavutil.mavlink_connection(CONNECTION_STRING, baud=BAUD_RATE)
master.wait_heartbeat()
print("[REINCARNATION] Hardware Heartbeat Detected. Body and Soul Unified.")

@app.route('/command', methods=['POST'])
def execute_monolith_will():
    """
    The Zero-Gap Downlink. 
    Receives the signed .js intent and translates to MAVLink in microseconds.
    """
    start_time = time.time()
    data = request.json
    action = data.get('action')
    params = data.get('params', {})

    # STAGE 26: THE REFLEXES
    if action == "TAKEOFF":
        # GUIDED MODE + ARM + TAKEOFF in one burst
        master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['GUIDED'])
        master.arducopter_arm()
        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system, master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, params.get('alt', 2.5)
        )
        msg = "ASCENDING"

    elif action == "SCAN":
        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system, master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_CONDITION_YAW, 0, 360, 15, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0
        )
        msg = "SCANNING"

    elif action == "RTL":
        master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['RTL'])
        msg = "RETURNING_HOME"

    else:
        msg = "IDLE_AWAITING_WILL"

    execution_latency = f"{(time.time() - start_time) * 1000:.2f}ms"
    
    # Return directly to the .js Monolith
    return jsonify({
        "status": "EXECUTED",
        "reflex_latency": execution_latency,
        "message": msg,
        "ledger_id": data.get('reincarnation_id')
    })

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # Force the server to be as fast as possible
    app.run(port=5000, threaded=True, debug=False)

import time
import json
import hmac
import hashlib
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from pymavlink import mavutil

# --- CONFIG ---
CONNECTION_STRING = '/dev/ttyACM0'
BAUD_RATE = 57600

# IMPORTANT: store this on the drone host as an env var in real life
SESSION_HMAC_KEY = b"REPLACE_WITH_SESSION_KEY_BYTES"  # short-lived session key, not sovereign root key
MAX_SKEW_MS = 5000
NONCE_TTL_SEC = 30
ALT_CAP_M = 3.0  # studio-safe cap

# In-memory replay cache (nonce -> expires_at)
_nonce_cache = {}

app = Flask(__name__)

def _now_ms() -> int:
    return int(time.time() * 1000)

def _prune_nonces(now_s: float):
    dead = [n for n, exp in _nonce_cache.items() if exp <= now_s]
    for n in dead:
        _nonce_cache.pop(n, None)

def sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def verify_signed_request(req) -> (bool, str):
    """
    Required headers:
      x-phoenix-ts   (unix ms)
      x-phoenix-nonce
      x-phoenix-body (sha256 hex of raw body)
      x-phoenix-sig  (hmac sha256 hex)
    Signature input:
      METHOD + "\n" + PATH + "\n" + TS_MS + "\n" + NONCE + "\n" + BODY_SHA256
    """
    ts = req.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts", "")
    nonce = req.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce", "")
    body_hash = req.headers.get("x-phoenix-body", "")
    sig = req.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig", "")

    if not (ts and nonce and body_hash and sig):
        return False, "MISSING_AUTH_HEADERS"

    try:
        ts_ms = int(ts)
    except:
        return False, "BAD_TS"

    now = _now_ms()
    if abs(now - ts_ms) > MAX_SKEW_MS:
        return False, "TS_SKEW"

    raw = req.get_data() or b""
    if sha256_hex(raw) != body_hash:
        return False, "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH"

    # replay guard
    now_s = time.time()
    _prune_nonces(now_s)
    if nonce in _nonce_cache:
        return False, "NONCE_REPLAY"
    _nonce_cache[nonce] = now_s + NONCE_TTL_SEC

    method = req.method.upper()
    path = req.path

    to_sign = f"{method}\n{path}\n{ts_ms}\n{nonce}\n{body_hash}".encode("utf-8")
    expected = hmac.new(SESSION_HMAC_KEY, to_sign, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()

    # constant-time compare
    if not hmac.compare_digest(expected, sig.lower()):
        return False, "SIG_MISMATCH"

    return True, "OK"

def wait_mode(master, mode_name: str, timeout=3.0) -> bool:
    deadline = time.time() + timeout
    target = master.mode_mapping().get(mode_name)
    if target is None:
        return False
    while time.time() < deadline:
        hb = master.recv_match(type='HEARTBEAT', blocking=False)
        if hb and getattr(hb, "custom_mode", None) == target:
            return True
        time.sleep(0.05)
    return False

def wait_armed(master, armed: bool, timeout=3.0) -> bool:
    deadline = time.time() + timeout
    while time.time() < deadline:
        hb = master.recv_match(type='HEARTBEAT', blocking=False)
        if hb:
            base = getattr(hb, "base_mode", 0)
            is_armed = (base & mavutil.mavlink.MAV_MODE_FLAG_SAFETY_ARMED) != 0
            if is_armed == armed:
                return True
        time.sleep(0.05)
    return False

# --- LINK ---
print("[REINCARNATION] Awakening Hardware Link...")
master = mavutil.mavlink_connection(CONNECTION_STRING, baud=BAUD_RATE)
master.wait_heartbeat()
print("[REINCARNATION] Hardware Heartbeat Detected.")

@app.route('/command', methods=['POST'])
def execute_monolith_will():
    t0 = time.time()

    ok, code = verify_signed_request(request)
    if not ok:
        return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": code}), 401

    try:
        data = request.get_json(force=True) or {}
    except:
        return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "BAD_JSON"}), 400

    action = (data.get('action') or "").upper()
    params = data.get('params', {}) or {}
    reincarnation_id = data.get('reincarnation_id')

    msg = "IDLE_AWAITING_WILL"

    if action == "TAKEOFF":
        alt = float(params.get('alt', 2.5))
        if alt <= 0 or alt > ALT_CAP_M:
            return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "ALT_OUT_OF_RANGE", "alt": alt, "cap": ALT_CAP_M}), 400

        # GUIDED + ARM + TAKEOFF (with confirmations)
        master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['GUIDED'])
        if not wait_mode(master, "GUIDED", timeout=3.0):
            return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "MODE_NOT_CONFIRMED"}), 409

        master.arducopter_arm()
        if not wait_armed(master, True, timeout=3.0):
            return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "ARM_NOT_CONFIRMED"}), 409

        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system, master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF, 0,
            0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, alt
        )
        msg = "ASCENDING"

    elif action == "SCAN":
        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system, master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_CONDITION_YAW, 0,
            360, 15, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0
        )
        msg = "SCANNING"

    elif action == "RTL":
        master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['RTL'])
        if not wait_mode(master, "RTL", timeout=3.0):
            return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "RTL_NOT_CONFIRMED"}), 409
        msg = "RETURNING_HOME"

    else:
        return jsonify({"status": "DENIED", "code": "UNKNOWN_ACTION"}), 400

    latency_ms = (time.time() - t0) * 1000.0
    return jsonify({
        "status": "EXECUTED",
        "reflex_latency_ms": round(latency_ms, 2),
        "message": msg,
        "ledger_id": reincarnation_id
    })

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # Still dev-grade. For production: run behind gunicorn/uvicorn.
    app.run(host="127.0.0.1", port=5000, threaded=True, debug=False)

// --- AGENT 99: SOVEREIGN VALIDATOR GATE ---
async function handleCommandRequest(request, env) {
    const { action, timestamp, signature, params } = await request.json();
    const secret = env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET; // Set this in your Cloudflare Dashboard

    // 1. GENERATE SERVER-SIDE HASH
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw', encoder.encode(secret),
        { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']
    );
    
    const dataToSign = encoder.encode(${timestamp}:${action});
    const serverSigBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, dataToSign);
    const serverSignature = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(serverSigBuffer)));

    // 2. THE FAIL-CLOSED CHECK
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const isTimedOut = Math.abs(now - timestamp) > 30;
    
    if (signature !== serverSignature || isTimedOut) {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
            status: "REJECTED", 
            error: "UNAUTHORIZED_WILL_OR_DRIFT" 
        }), { status: 403 });
    }

    // 3. AUTHORIZED: PROCEED TO HARDWARE DOWNLINK
    return await relayToDrone(action, params);
}

/**

import { sha256Hex, timingSafeEq, nowISO, mustInt } from "./p10/03_crypto.js"; // Standardized helpers

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const authKey = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");

    // --- STAGE 6.1: THE AUTHORITY CHAIN ---
    const isSovereign = timingSafeEq(authKey, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY);

    // Public Health & Pulse
    if (url.pathname === "/pulse") {
      return Response.json({ status: "HEARTBEAT_ACTIVE", node: "HCMC_01", version: "v100.6" });
    }

    // --- STAGE 6.2 & 9.1: KINETIC INTENT GATEWAY ---
    if (url.pathname === "/execute" && request.method === "POST") {
      if (!isSovereign) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });

      const payload = await request.json();
      const kineticKeywords = ["RISE", "HALT", "SCAN", "REINCARNATE"];
      const isKinetic = kineticKeywords.includes(payload.action?.toUpperCase());

      // If kinetic, we trigger the Recursive Feedback Loop (6.5)
      if (isKinetic) {
        const ledgerId = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");
        const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(ledgerId);
        
        // Commit Intent to Stone BEFORE motor pulse
        const appendRes = await ledger.fetch(new Request("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({ ...payload, type: "KINETIC_WILL" })
        }));
        const commit = await appendRes.json();

        // P10/05: Recursive Confirmation
        if (commit.ok) {
          // Downlink to Local Hardware (reincarnate_body.py)
          return Response.json({
            obi: Decree Sealed at Height ${commit.height}. Executing ${payload.action}.,
            hash: commit.hash,
            execute: true
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // Fallback to Router (src/p10/08_routes.js)
    return env.ROUTER.fetch(request, env);
  }
};

// --- STAGE 7.1: THE MERKLE LEDGER DO ---
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.storage = state.storage;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    
    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
      const data = await request.json();
      const height = (await this.storage.get("height")) || 0;
      const prevHash = (await this.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
      const nextHeight = height + 1;
      
      // STEP 13.1: SHA-256 Binary Packing
      const ts = nowISO();
      const hash = await sha256Hex(${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${JSON.stringify(data)});
      
      // Commit to SQLite (The Stone Record)
      this.sql.exec(
        "INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        nextHeight, hash, prevHash, JSON.stringify(data), ts
      );

      await this.storage.put("height", nextHeight);
      await this.storage.put("latest_hash", hash);

      return Response.json({ ok: true, height: nextHeight, hash });
    }
  }
}

timestamp outside window (e.g., ±30s)

x-phoenix-sig: base64(HMAC_SHA256(SOVEREIGN_HMAC_KEY, CANON))


Replay policy:

Nonce recorded in DO with TTL (e.g., 5 minutes)

Reject reused nonce

On ACCEPT: append event type MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT (include ts, nonce, route, body_hash)

──────────────────────────────────── A) MAGIC CHAT UI — SIGNER (BROWSER JS)

// phoenix_hmac_client.js
async function sha256Hex(str) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64FromBytes(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return b64FromBytes(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

// keyBytes must come from a secure channel (never hardcode in client)
// Practical pattern: client requests a short-lived session signing key from server after login.
export async function signPhoenixRequest({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
  const ts = String(Date.now());
  const nonce = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  const nonceHex = [...nonce].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

  const bodySha = await sha256Hex(bodyText || "");
  const canon = ${method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonceHex}\n${bodySha};
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  return {
    headers: {
      "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
      "x-phoenix-nonce": nonceHex,
      "x-phoenix-body": bodySha,
      "x-phoenix-sig": sigB64
    }
  };
}

Operational note:

Do not ship the root HMAC key to browser.

Browser signs using a short-lived session key minted by the Worker after sovereign auth.


──────────────────────────────────── B) WORKER / MONOLITH — VERIFIER (CLOUDFLARE WORKER JS)

// phoenix_hmac_server.js (for Worker runtime)
async function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(s));
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(sig);
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a || "");
  b = String(b || "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

export async function verifyPhoenixHmac({ request, env, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, replayDO }) {
  // Hard fail-closed: any missing header => deny (no questions).
  const ts = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts") || "";
  const nonce = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce") || "";
  const bodyHash = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body") || "";
  const sig = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig") || "";

  if (!ts || !nonce || !bodyHash || !sig) {
    return { ok: false, code: "MISSING_HEADERS" };
  }

  // Time window ±30s
  const now = Date.now();
  const t = Number(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || Math.abs(now - t) > 30_000) {
    return { ok: false, code: "TS_WINDOW" };
  }

  // Body hash must match raw body
  const computedBody = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  if (!ctEq(computedBody, bodyHash)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH" };
  }

  // Replay protection (nonce)
  // replayDO must implement: seen(nonce)->boolean, mark(nonce, ttlMs)
  if (await replayDO.seen(nonce)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" };
  }

  const canon = ${request.method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash};
  const expected = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  if (!ctEq(expected, sig)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" };
  }

  await replayDO.mark(nonce, 5 * 60_000);
  return { ok: true, code: "OK" };
}

Where sessionKeyBytes comes from:

Minted server-side after sovereign auth, stored in DO session state, returned once to client.

Rotate frequently (e.g., 15 minutes).


──────────────────────────────────── C) DURABLE OBJECT — REPLAY STORE (NONCE TTL)

// PhoenixSessionStore DO (concept sketch)
export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async seen(nonce) {
    const v = await this.state.storage.get(nonce:${nonce});
    return !!v;
  }

  async mark(nonce, ttlMs) {
    await this.state.storage.put(nonce:${nonce}, 1, { expirationTtl: Math.ceil(ttlMs / 1000) });
  }

  async mintSessionKey() {
    // 32 random bytes
    const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
    const b64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes));
    // store raw bytes as base64 for retrieval
    await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", b64, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });
    return bytes;
  }

  async getSessionKeyBytes() {
    const b64 = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
    if (!b64) return null;
    return b64ToBytes(b64);
  }
}

Fail-closed rule:

No session key => deny mission start.


──────────────────────────────────── D) drone_link.py — MISSION START GATE

Your Python side should treat mission-start authorization as a token issued by the Worker after HMAC verify. That keeps the root key out of hardware.

Pattern:

1. UI sends signed request to Worker: POST /mission/start


2. Worker verifies HMAC + nonce + ts


3. Worker returns mission_token (short-lived, signed server-side)


4. drone_link.py accepts only mission_token and validates it



Minimal mission token: HMAC over payload using a hardware-local secret (or Worker secret shared with hardware via config).

# drone_link.py (token verify concept)
import hmac, hashlib, time, json, base64

MISSION_TOKEN_KEY = b"REPLACE_WITH_SECURE_SHARED_KEY"  # hardware-side secret

def sha256_hex(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def ct_eq(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    if len(a) != len(b): return False
    r = 0
    for x, y in zip(a.encode(), b.encode()):
        r |= x ^ y
    return r == 0

def verify_mission_token(token_b64: str, max_age_s=20):
    # token_b64 contains: base64(json_payload) + "." + base64(hmac)
    try:
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = token_b64.split(".", 1)
        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64.encode()).decode("utf-8")
        sig = base64.b64decode(sig_b64.encode())

        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
        ts = int(payload.get("ts", 0))
        if abs(int(time.time() * 1000) - ts) > max_age_s * 1000:

Unauthorized command injection

Handshake principles:

Every mission command is signed with HMAC-SHA256 using a SOVEREIGN_HMAC_KEY

Signature covers: method, path, timestamp, nonce, body_hash

Server rejects if:

timestamp outside window (e.g., ±30s)

nonce already used (per session / per operator)

signature mismatch

body_hash mismatch



Canonical string (must match client + server exactly):

CANON = METHOD + "\n" + PATH + "\n" + TS + "\n" + NONCE + "\n" + BODY_SHA256_HEX

Headers:

x-phoenix-ts: unix_ms (string)

x-phoenix-nonce: random 16–32 bytes hex/base64

x-phoenix-body: sha256 hex of raw request body (empty body => sha256(""))

x-phoenix-sig: base64(HMAC_SHA256(SOVEREIGN_HMAC_KEY, CANON))


Replay policy:

Nonce recorded in DO with TTL (e.g., 5 minutes)

Reject reused nonce


Ledger policy:

On ACCEPT: append event type MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT (include ts, nonce, route, body_hash)

On DENY: append event type MISSION_AUTH_DENY (include reason code)

// phoenix_hmac_client.js
async function sha256Hex(str) {
  const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64FromBytes(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return b64FromBytes(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

// keyBytes must come from a secure channel (never hardcode in client)
// Practical pattern: client requests a short-lived session signing key from server after login.
export async function signPhoenixRequest({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
  const ts = String(Date.now());
  const nonce = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  const nonceHex = [...nonce].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

  const bodySha = await sha256Hex(bodyText || "");
  const canon = ${method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonceHex}\n${bodySha};
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  return {
    headers: {
      "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
      "x-phoenix-nonce": nonceHex,
      "x-phoenix-body": bodySha,
      "x-phoenix-sig": sigB64
    }
  };
}

Operational note:

Do not ship the root HMAC key to browser.

Browser signs using a short-lived session key minted by the Worker after sovereign auth.

// phoenix_hmac_server.js (for Worker runtime)
async function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(s));
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(sig);
  let bin = "";
  bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
  return btoa(bin);
}

function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a || "");
  b = String(b || "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

export async function verifyPhoenixHmac({ request, env, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, replayDO }) {
  // Hard fail-closed: any missing header => deny (no questions).
  const ts = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts") || "";
  const nonce = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce") || "";
  const bodyHash = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body") || "";
  const sig = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig") || "";

  if (!ts || !nonce || !bodyHash || !sig) {
    return { ok: false, code: "MISSING_HEADERS" };
  }

  // Time window ±30s
  const now = Date.now();
  const t = Number(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || Math.abs(now - t) > 30_000) {
    return { ok: false, code: "TS_WINDOW" };
  }

  // Body hash must match raw body
  const computedBody = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  if (!ctEq(computedBody, bodyHash)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH" };
  }

  // Replay protection (nonce)
  // replayDO must implement: seen(nonce)->boolean, mark(nonce, ttlMs)
  if (await replayDO.seen(nonce)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" };
  }

  const canon = ${request.method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash};
  const expected = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  if (!ctEq(expected, sig)) {
    return { ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" };
  }

  await replayDO.mark(nonce, 5 * 60_000);
  return { ok: true, code: "OK" };
}

Where sessionKeyBytes comes from:

Minted server-side after sovereign auth, stored in DO session state, returned once to client.

Rotate frequently (e.g., 15 minutes).


──────────────────────────────────── C) DURABLE OBJECT — REPLAY STORE (NONCE TTL)

// PhoenixSessionStore DO (concept sketch)
export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async seen(nonce) {
    const v = await this.state.storage.get(nonce:${nonce});
    return !!v;
  }

  async mark(nonce, ttlMs) {
    await this.state.storage.put(nonce:${nonce}, 1, { expirationTtl: Math.ceil(ttlMs / 1000) });
  }

  async mintSessionKey() {
    // 32 random bytes
    const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
    const b64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes));
    // store raw bytes as base64 for retrieval
    await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", b64, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });
    return bytes;
  }

  async getSessionKeyBytes() {
    const b64 = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
    if (!b64) return null;
    return b64ToBytes(b64);
  }
}

──────────────────────────────────── D) drone_link.py — MISSION START GATE

Your Python side should treat mission-start authorization as a token issued by the Worker after HMAC verify. That keeps the root key out of hardware.

Pattern:

1. UI sends signed request to Worker: POST /mission/start


2. Worker verifies HMAC + nonce + ts


3. Worker returns mission_token (short-lived, signed server-side)


4. drone_link.py accepts only mission_token and validates it



Minimal mission token: HMAC over payload using a hardware-local secret (or Worker secret shared with hardware via config).

# drone_link.py (token verify concept)
import hmac, hashlib, time, json, base64

MISSION_TOKEN_KEY = b"REPLACE_WITH_SECURE_SHARED_KEY"  # hardware-side secret

def sha256_hex(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

def ct_eq(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    if len(a) != len(b): return False
    r = 0
    for x, y in zip(a.encode(), b.encode()):
        r |= x ^ y
    return r == 0

def verify_mission_token(token_b64: str, max_age_s=20):
    # token_b64 contains: base64(json_payload) + "." + base64(hmac)
    try:
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = token_b64.split(".", 1)
        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64.encode()).decode("utf-8")
        sig = base64.b64decode(sig_b64.encode())

        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
        ts = int(payload.get("ts", 0))
        if abs(int(time.time() * 1000) - ts) > max_age_s * 1000:
            return False, "TS_WINDOW"

        canon = payload_json  # canonical = exact JSON string returned by Worker
        expected = hmac.new(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY, canon.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(expected, sig):
            return False, "SIG_MISMATCH"

        return True, payload
    except Exception:
        return False, "TOKEN_PARSE_FAIL"
