```js
// phoenix_client_signer_v2.js
export async function sha256HexUtf8(text) {
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(String(text ?? "")));
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64FromBytes(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", keyBytes, { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]);
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return b64FromBytes(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

/**
 * signPhoenixRequestV2
 * Canon string:
 *   METHOD\nPATH\nTS_MS\nNONCE_HEX\nBODY_SHA256_HEX
 *
 * Headers:
 *   x-phoenix-ts, x-phoenix-nonce, x-phoenix-body, x-phoenix-sig
 */
export async function signPhoenixRequestV2({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
  const ts = String(Date.now()); // unix ms
  const nonceBytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
  const nonceHex = [...nonceBytes].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

  const bodySha = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  const canon = `${String(method).toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonceHex}\n${bodySha}`;
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  return {
    ts, nonceHex, bodySha, sigB64,
    headers: {
      "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
      "x-phoenix-nonce": nonceHex,
      "x-phoenix-body": bodySha,
      "x-phoenix-sig": sigB64
    }
  };
}
B) Worker (Edge / Monolith) — verifies, logs ACCEPT/DENY, mints mission_token
// phoenix_hmac_verify_v2.js
export async function sha256HexUtf8(text) {
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(String(text ?? "")));
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

function bytesToB64(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", keyBytes, { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]);
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return bytesToB64(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a || "");
  b = String(b || "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

/**
 * verifyPhoenixHmacV2(request, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, sessionDO)
 * - fail-closed on missing headers
 * - ts window ±30s
 * - body hash binding
 * - nonce replay guard in DO (TTL 5m)
 */
export async function verifyPhoenixHmacV2({ request, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, sessionDO }) {
  const ts = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts") || "";
  const nonce = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce") || "";
  const bodyHash = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body") || "";
  const sig = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig") || "";

  if (!ts || !nonce || !bodyHash || !sig) return { ok: false, code: "MISSING_HEADERS" };

  const now = Date.now();
  const t = Number(ts);
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || Math.abs(now - t) > 30_000) return { ok: false, code: "TS_WINDOW" };

  const computedBody = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  if (!ctEq(computedBody, bodyHash)) return { ok: false, code: "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH" };

  // nonce replay check
  const seenRes = await sessionDO.fetch("https://session/nonce/seen", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ nonce }) });
  const seen = await seenRes.json();
  if (seen.seen) return { ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" };

  const canon = `${request.method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash}`;
  const expected = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);
  if (!ctEq(expected, sig)) return { ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" };

  await sessionDO.fetch("https://session/nonce/mark", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ nonce, ttl_s: 300 }) });
  return { ok: true, code: "OK" };
}

/**
 * mintMissionTokenV2(payloadObj, env.MISSION_TOKEN_KEY)
 * token = base64(payload_json) + "." + base64(hmac(payload_json))
 * payload must include ts_ms and expires_ms window checked by hardware.
 */
export async function mintMissionTokenV2(payloadObj, missionTokenKeyB64) {
  const keyBytes = b64ToBytes(missionTokenKeyB64);
  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, payloadJson);
  const payloadB64 = bytesToB64(new TextEncoder().encode(payloadJson));
  return `${payloadB64}.${sigB64}`;
}
C) Durable Object — Session Key + Nonce Store (TTL)
// PhoenixSessionStoreDO_v2.js
export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/session/mint") {
      // 32 random bytes, stored for 15m
      const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const b64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes));
      await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", b64, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });
      return Response.json({ ok: true, sessionKeyB64: b64, ttl_s: 900 });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/session/get") {
      const b64 = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
      if (!b64) return new Response("NO_SESSION_KEY", { status: 401 });
      return Response.json({ ok: true, sessionKeyB64: b64 });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/nonce/seen") {
      const { nonce } = await request.json();
      const v = await this.state.storage.get(`nonce:${nonce}`);
      return Response.json({ ok: true, seen: !!v });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/nonce/mark") {
      const { nonce, ttl_s } = await request.json();
      const ttl = Math.max(1, Math.min(3600, Number(ttl_s || 300)));
      await this.state.storage.put(`nonce:${nonce}`, 1, { expirationTtl: ttl });
      return Response.json({ ok: true });
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
}

// RomeSovereignState_v2.js
import { verifyPhoenixHmacV2, sha256HexUtf8 } from "./phoenix_hmac_verify_v2.js";

export class RomeSovereignState {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
    this.memory = null; // restored lazily
  }

  async _init() {
    if (this.memory) return;
    const stored = await this.state.storage.get("rome_memory");
    this.memory = stored || {
      last_intent: null,
      drone_status: "STANDBY",
      ledger_sync_id: 0,
      sovereign_coordinates: { lat: 10.7626, lon: 106.6601 }
    };
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    await this._init();
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // READ is allowed (optionally sign-gate later); WRITE is always sign-gated.
    if (url.pathname === "/get-reality") {
      return Response.json({ ok: true, memory: this.memory });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/lock-intent") {
      const bodyText = await request.text(); // verify against exact raw
      const session = this.env.SESSIONS.get(this.env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));

      // session key must exist; verifier will fail-closed otherwise
      const keyRes = await session.fetch("https://session/session/get");
      if (!keyRes.ok) return new Response("NO_SESSION_KEY", { status: 401 });
      const { sessionKeyB64 } = await keyRes.json();
      const sessionKeyBytes = (function b64ToBytes(b64){
        const bin = atob(b64); const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
        return out;
      })(sessionKeyB64);

      const v = await verifyPhoenixHmacV2({
        request,
        path: "/lock-intent",
        bodyText,
        sessionKeyBytes,
        sessionDO: session
      });
      if (!v.ok) return new Response(`UNAUTHORIZED:${v.code}`, { status: 401 });

      const data = JSON.parse(bodyText || "{}");
      this.memory.last_intent = data.intent || null;
      this.memory.ledger_sync_id = (this.memory.ledger_sync_id || 0) + 1;
      await this.state.storage.put("rome_memory", this.memory);

      return Response.json({ ok: true, status: "LOCKED", sync_id: this.memory.ledger_sync_id });
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
}

# reincarnate_body_v2.py (core pieces only)
import time, json, base64, hmac, hashlib
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify

app = Flask(__name__)

# Shared secret with Worker (NOT the sovereign root key)
MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64 = "REPLACE_ME"  # env var in real life
MISSION_TOKEN_KEY = base64.b64decode(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64)

MAX_SAFE_ALT_M = 3.0
MAX_TOKEN_AGE_MS = 20_000

def ct_eq(a: bytes, b: bytes) -> bool:
    return hmac.compare_digest(a, b)

def verify_mission_token(token: str):
    try:
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = token.split(".", 1)
        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")
        sig = base64.b64decode(sig_b64.encode("utf-8"))

        now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
        ts_ms = int(payload.get("ts_ms", 0))
        exp_ms = int(payload.get("exp_ms", 0))

        if ts_ms <= 0 or exp_ms <= 0: return False, "TOKEN_TS_MISSING", None
        if now_ms > exp_ms or abs(now_ms - ts_ms) > MAX_TOKEN_AGE_MS: return False, "TOKEN_EXPIRED", None

        expected = hmac.new(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY, payload_json.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
        if not ct_eq(expected, sig): return False, "TOKEN_SIG_MISMATCH", None

        return True, "OK", payload
    except Exception:
        return False, "TOKEN_PARSE_FAIL", None

def safety_gate(action: str, params: dict):
    # hard caps for studio
    if action == "TAKEOFF":
        alt = float(params.get("alt", 2.5))
        if alt > MAX_SAFE_ALT_M: return False, "ALT_CAP"
        if alt <= 0.2: return False, "ALT_TOO_LOW"
    return True, "OK"

@app.route("/command", methods=["POST"])
def command():
    start = time.time()
    data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}

    token = data.get("mission_token", "")
    ok, code, payload = verify_mission_token(token)
    if not ok:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "status": "DENY", "code": code}), 401

    action = (payload.get("action") or data.get("action") or "").upper()
    params = payload.get("params") or data.get("params") or {}

    ok2, code2 = safety_gate(action, params)
    if not ok2:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "status": "DENY", "code": code2}), 403

    # TODO: call MAVLink here (kept out of this snippet on purpose)
    # execute(action, params)

    ms = (time.time() - start) * 1000.0
    return jsonify({
        "ok": True,
        "status": "EXECUTED",
        "action": action,
        "reflex_latency_ms": round(ms, 2),
        "token_jti": payload.get("jti"),
        "ts_ms": payload.get("ts_ms"),
    })

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(host="127.0.0.1", port=5000, threaded=True, debug=False)

Minimal route skeleton:

// inside fetch() router
if (url.pathname === "/mission/start" && request.method === "POST") {
  const bodyText = await request.text();
  const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));

  // load session key
  const keyRes = await session.fetch("https://session/session/get");
  if (!keyRes.ok) return new Response("NO_SESSION_KEY", { status: 401 });
  const { sessionKeyB64 } = await keyRes.json();
  const sessionKeyBytes = (function(b64){
    const bin = atob(b64), out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (let i=0;i<bin.length;i++) out[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
    return out;
  })(sessionKeyB64);

  const v = await verifyPhoenixHmacV2({
    request, path: "/mission/start", bodyText, sessionKeyBytes, sessionDO: session
  });

  // ledger log (accept/deny)
  const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"));
  if (!v.ok) {
    await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ type:"MISSION_AUTH_DENY", code:v.code, ts:Date.now() })
    });
    return new Response(`UNAUTHORIZED:${v.code}`, { status: 401 });
  }

  const req = JSON.parse(bodyText || "{}");
  const ts_ms = Date.now();
  const exp_ms = ts_ms + 20_000;
  const jti = crypto.randomUUID();

  const token = await mintMissionTokenV2(
    { ts_ms, exp_ms, jti, action: req.action, params: req.params || {} },
    env.MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64
  );

  await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({ type:"MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT", ts:ts_ms, jti, action:req.action })
  });

  return Response.json({ ok:true, mission_token: token, jti, exp_ms });
}

# reincarnate_body_v2.py (Safety Gate + Actuator)
import time, json, base64, hmac, hashlib
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify

app = Flask(__name__)

# Shared secret with Worker (Provisioned via env or secure config)
MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64 = "REPLACE_WITH_B64_KEY_FROM_CLOUDFLARE_ENV"
MISSION_TOKEN_KEY = base64.b64decode(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64)

MAX_SAFE_ALT_M = 3.0
MAX_TOKEN_AGE_MS = 20_000

def ct_eq(a: bytes, b: bytes) -> bool:
    return hmac.compare_digest(a, b)

def verify_mission_token(token: str):
    try:
        payload_b64, sig_b64 = token.split(".", 1)
        payload_json = base64.b64decode(payload_b64.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")
        sig = base64.b64decode(sig_b64.encode("utf-8"))

        now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
        payload = json.loads(payload_json)
        ts_ms = int(payload.get("ts_ms", 0))
        exp_ms = int(payload.get("exp_ms", 0))

        if now_ms > exp_ms or abs(now_ms - ts_ms) > MAX_TOKEN_AGE_MS: 
            return False, "TOKEN_EXPIRED", None

        expected = hmac.new(MISSION_TOKEN_KEY, payload_json.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).digest()
        if not ct_eq(expected, sig): 
            return False, "TOKEN_SIG_MISMATCH", None

        return True, "OK", payload
    except Exception:
        return False, "TOKEN_PARSE_FAIL", None

def safety_gate(action: str, params: dict):
    if action == "TAKEOFF":
        alt = float(params.get("alt", 2.5))
        if alt > MAX_SAFE_ALT_M: return False, f"ALT_CAP_VIOLATION:{alt}m"
    return True, "OK"

@app.route("/command", methods=["POST"])
def command():
    data = request.get_json(force=True, silent=True) or {}
    token = data.get("mission_token", "")
    
    ok, code, payload = verify_mission_token(token)
    if not ok:
        return jsonify({"ok": False, "status": "DENY", "code": code}), 401

    action = payload.get("action", "").upper()
    params = payload.get("params", {})

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/pulse" && request.method === "GET") {
      return Response.json({ ok: true, node: "HCMC_01", version: env.VERSION || "dev", time_ms: Date.now() });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/session/mint" && request.method === "POST") {
      // sovereign-only gate
      const k = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";
      if (!timingSafeEq(k, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) return new Response("DENY", { status: 401 });

      const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
      const res = await session.fetch("https://session/session/mint", { method: "POST" });
      return res; // {sessionKeyB64, exp_ms}
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/mission/start" && request.method === "POST") {
      const bodyText = await request.text();
      const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
      const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"));

      // obtain sessionKeyBytes from DO (metadata only if you choose; but easiest: DO does verify internally)
      // Preferred: session DO exposes /session/verify that returns {ok, code} and handles nonce TTL itself.

      const v = await verifyPhoenixHmacV2({ request, path: "/mission/start", bodyText, sessionDO: session });
      if (!v.ok) {
        await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", { method:"POST", body: JSON.stringify({ type:"MISSION_AUTH_DENY", ts_ms: Date.now(), code:v.code, route:"/mission/start" }) });
        return new Response(`UNAUTHORIZED:${v.code}`, { status: 401 });
      }

      const req = JSON.parse(bodyText || "{}");
      const ts_ms = Date.now();
      const exp_ms = ts_ms + 20_000;
      const jti = crypto.randomUUID();

      const tokenPayload = { ts_ms, exp_ms, jti, action: req.action, params: req.params || {} };
      const mission_token = await mintMissionTokenV2(tokenPayload, env.MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64);

      await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", { method:"POST", body: JSON.stringify({ type:"MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT", ts_ms, exp_ms, jti, action: req.action, route:"/mission/start" }) });

      return Response.json({ ok: true, mission_token, jti, exp_ms });
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
};

PhoenixSessionStore DO v2 — /session/mint, /session/status, /verify

// PhoenixSessionStoreDO_v2.js
"use strict";

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}
function bytesToB64(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}
function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a ?? "");
  b = String(b ?? "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return bytesToB64(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // POST /session/mint  -> returns sessionKeyB64 once (15m TTL)
    if (url.pathname === "/session/mint" && request.method === "POST") {
      const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const sessionKeyB64 = bytesToB64(bytes);
      const exp_ms = Date.now() + 15 * 60_000;

      await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", sessionKeyB64, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });
      await this.state.storage.put("sessionExpMs", exp_ms, { expirationTtl: 15 * 60 });

      return Response.json({ ok: true, sessionKeyB64, exp_ms });
    }

    // GET /session/status -> metadata only
    if (url.pathname === "/session/status" && request.method === "GET") {
      const exp_ms = (await this.state.storage.get("sessionExpMs")) || null;
      const hasKey = !!(await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64"));
      return Response.json({ ok: true, hasKey, exp_ms });
    }

    // POST /verify -> verifies V2 signature + nonce replay in same DO
    // body: { method, path, ts, nonce, bodyHash, sig }
    if (url.pathname === "/verify" && request.method === "POST") {
      const data = (await request.json().catch(() => null)) || {};
      const method = String(data.method || "").toUpperCase();
      const path = String(data.path || "");
      const ts = String(data.ts || "");
      const nonce = String(data.nonce || "");
      const bodyHash = String(data.bodyHash || "");
      const sig = String(data.sig || "");

      if (!method || !path || !ts || !nonce || !bodyHash || !sig) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "MISSING_FIELDS" }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const t = Number(ts);
      const now = Date.now();
      if (!Number.isFinite(t) || Math.abs(now - t) > 30_000) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "TS_WINDOW" }, { status: 401 });
      }

      const sessionKeyB64 = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
      if (!sessionKeyB64) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "NO_SESSION_KEY" }, { status: 401 });
      }

      // Replay guard (5 minutes)
      const seen = await this.state.storage.get(`nonce:${nonce}`);
      if (seen) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" }, { status: 401 });
      }

      const keyBytes = b64ToBytes(sessionKeyB64);
      const canon = `${method}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash}`;
      const expected = await hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, canon);

      if (!ctEq(expected, sig)) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" }, { status: 401 });
      }

      await this.state.storage.put(`nonce:${nonce}`, 1, { expirationTtl: 300 });
      return Response.json({ ok: true, code: "OK" });
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
}
Worker route integration — /session/mint, /session/status, /mission/start

// worker_routes_v2.js
"use strict";

function timingSafeEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a ?? "");
  b = String(b ?? "");
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

async function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(s ?? ""));
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}
function bytesToB64(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return bytesToB64(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

async function mintMissionTokenV2(payloadObj, missionKeyB64) {
  const keyBytes = b64ToBytes(missionKeyB64);
  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payloadObj); // canonical = exact string you sign
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, payloadJson);
  const payloadB64 = bytesToB64(new TextEncoder().encode(payloadJson));
  return `${payloadB64}.${sigB64}`;
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/pulse" && request.method === "GET") {
      return Response.json({ ok: true, node: "HCMC_01", version: env.VERSION || "v2", time_ms: Date.now() });
    }

    // /session/mint (sovereign-gated)
    if (url.pathname === "/session/mint" && request.method === "POST") {
      const k = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";
      if (!timingSafeEq(k, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) return new Response("DENY", { status: 401 });

      const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
      return session.fetch("https://session/session/mint", { method: "POST" });
    }

    // /session/status (sovereign-gated, metadata only)
    if (url.pathname === "/session/status" && request.method === "GET") {
      const k = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";
      if (!timingSafeEq(k, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) return new Response("DENY", { status: 401 });

      const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
      return session.fetch("https://session/session/status", { method: "GET" });
    }

    // /mission/start (Handshake V2, verified by SESSIONS DO)
    if (url.pathname === "/mission/start" && request.method === "POST") {
      const bodyText = await request.text();
      const bodyHash = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText);

      // Extract V2 headers
      const ts = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts") || "";
      const nonce = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce") || "";
      const hdrBody = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body") || "";
      const sig = request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig") || "";

      const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
      const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"));

      // Fail-closed missing headers
      if (!ts || !nonce || !hdrBody || !sig) {
        await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({ type: "MISSION_AUTH_DENY", ts_ms: Date.now(), code: "MISSING_HEADERS", route: "/mission/start" })
        });
        return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED:MISSING_HEADERS", { status: 401 });
      }

      // Body binding must match
      if (!timingSafeEq(bodyHash, hdrBody)) {
        await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({ type: "MISSION_AUTH_DENY", ts_ms: Date.now(), code: "BODY_HASH_MISMATCH", route: "/mission/start" })
        });
        return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED:BODY_HASH_MISMATCH", { status: 401 });
      }

      // Verify inside SESSIONS DO (nonce + signature + ts window)
      const vRes = await session.fetch("https://session/verify", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          method: request.method,
          path: "/mission/start",
          ts,
          nonce,
          bodyHash: hdrBody,
          sig
        })
      });

      const v = await vRes.json().catch(() => ({ ok: false, code: "VERIFY_PARSE_FAIL" }));
      if (!v.ok) {
        await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({ type: "MISSION_AUTH_DENY", ts_ms: Date.now(), code: v.code || "VERIFY_FAIL", route: "/mission/start" })
        });
        return new Response(`UNAUTHORIZED:${v.code || "VERIFY_FAIL"}`, { status: 401 });
      }

      // Verified: mint mission token (20s TTL)
      const req = JSON.parse(bodyText || "{}");
      const ts_ms = Date.now();
      const exp_ms = ts_ms + 20_000;
      const jti = crypto.randomUUID();

      const tokenPayload = {
        ts_ms, exp_ms, jti,
        action: String(req.action || "").toUpperCase(),
        params: req.params || {}
      };

      const mission_token = await mintMissionTokenV2(tokenPayload, env.MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64);

      await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: "MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT", ts_ms, exp_ms, jti, action: tokenPayload.action, route: "/mission/start" })
      });

      return Response.json({ ok: true, mission_token, jti, exp_ms });
    }

    // keep /chat and /lock-intent as-is in your existing router; do not mix keys
    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
};

<!-- index.html (Throne Room) — Handshake V2 Mission Control -->
<script>
/**
 * CONFIG (set these)
 * - MONOLITH_BASE: your Cloudflare Worker base URL (no trailing slash)
 * - DRONE_BASE: your local/tunnel actuator base URL (no trailing slash) pointing at reincarnate_body_v2.py
 * - SOVEREIGN_KEY: NEVER commit this to git. Provide at runtime (prompt/localStorage/env-inject).
 */
const MONOLITH_BASE = "https://YOUR-WORKER.workers.dev";
const DRONE_BASE   = "https://YOUR-TUNNEL-TO-DRONE.example.com"; // e.g. https://xxxx.trycloudflare.com
let   SOVEREIGN_KEY = ""; // set via UI input, not hardcoded

// ---- helpers ----
function assert(cond, code) { if (!cond) throw new Error(code || "ASSERT_FAIL"); }

async function sha256HexUtf8(str) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(str ?? ""));
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

function bytesToB64(bytes) {
  let bin = "";
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(bin);
}
function b64ToBytes(b64) {
  const bin = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}
function randomNonceHex(lenBytes = 16) {
  const u8 = new Uint8Array(lenBytes);
  crypto.getRandomValues(u8);
  return [...u8].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    "raw",
    keyBytes,
    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
    false,
    ["sign"]
  );
  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
  return bytesToB64(new Uint8Array(sig));
}

/**
 * V2 signer: METHOD + "\n" + PATH + "\n" + TS_MS + "\n" + NONCE + "\n" + BODY_SHA256_HEX
 * Produces headers: x-phoenix-ts, x-phoenix-nonce, x-phoenix-body, x-phoenix-sig
 */
async function signPhoenixRequestV2({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
  const ts = String(Date.now());
  const nonce = randomNonceHex(16);
  const bodyHash = await sha256HexUtf8(bodyText || "");
  const canon = `${String(method).toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash}`;
  const sigB64 = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);

  return {
    headers: {
      "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
      "x-phoenix-nonce": nonce,
      "x-phoenix-body": bodyHash,
      "x-phoenix-sig": sigB64
    }
  };
}

// ---- session mint (sovereign-gated) ----
async function mintSessionKey() {
  assert(SOVEREIGN_KEY && SOVEREIGN_KEY.length > 0, "SOVEREIGN_KEY_MISSING");

  const res = await fetch(`${MONOLITH_BASE}/session/mint`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "x-sovereign-key": SOVEREIGN_KEY }
  });

  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`SESSION_MINT_FAIL:${res.status}`);
  const j = await res.json();
  assert(j && j.ok && j.sessionKeyB64, "SESSION_MINT_BAD_JSON");

  // IMPORTANT: this is ephemeral; keep in memory only
  return b64ToBytes(j.sessionKeyB64);
}

// ---- mission start -> token -> local command ----
async function startMission(action, params = {}) {
  const sessionKeyBytes = await mintSessionKey();

  // Worker expects signed request to /mission/start
  const body = JSON.stringify({ action, params });
  const signed = await signPhoenixRequestV2({
    method: "POST",
    path: "/mission/start",
    bodyText: body,
    sessionKeyBytes
  });

  const res = await fetch(`${MONOLITH_BASE}/mission/start`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { ...signed.headers, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body
  });

  const text = await res.text();
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`MISSION_START_DENY:${res.status}:${text.slice(0,120)}`);

  const j = JSON.parse(text);
  assert(j && j.ok && j.mission_token, "MISSION_START_BAD_JSON");

  // Forward token to local actuator
  const localRes = await fetch(`${DRONE_BASE}/command`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ mission_token: j.mission_token })
  });

  const localText = await localRes.text();
  if (!localRes.ok) throw new Error(`LOCAL_DENY:${localRes.status}:${localText.slice(0,120)}`);

  return { mission: j, local: JSON.parse(localText) };
}

// ---- minimal UI hooks (you can wire to your existing chat buttons) ----
window.PHOENIX = {
  setSovereignKey(k) { SOVEREIGN_KEY = String(k || ""); },
  async RISE(alt = 2.5)   { return startMission("TAKEOFF", { alt }); },
  async SCAN()            { return startMission("SCAN", {}); },
  async RTL()             { return startMission("RTL", {}); },
  async STATUS() {
    const r = await fetch(`${MONOLITH_BASE}/session/status`, { headers: { "x-sovereign-key": SOVEREIGN_KEY }});
    return r.json();
  }
};
</script>

<!-- Example controls (optional) -->
<div style="display:flex; gap:8px; align-items:center;">
  <input id="sk" placeholder="x-sovereign-key" type="password" style="width:320px;">
  <button onclick="PHOENIX.setSovereignKey(document.getElementById('sk').value)">Set Key</button>
  <button onclick="PHOENIX.RISE(2.5).then(console.log).catch(console.error)">RISE</button>
  <button onclick="PHOENIX.SCAN().then(console.log).catch(console.error)">SCAN</button>
  <button onclick="PHOENIX.RTL().then(console.log).catch(console.error)">RTL</button>
</div>

A) Throne Room (index.html) — mic button → POST audio blob

<button id="mic">🎙️ Hold to talk</button>
<script>
let rec, chunks = [], stream;

async function startRec() {
  stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
  rec = new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: "audio/webm" });
  chunks = [];
  rec.ondataavailable = (e) => e.data.size && chunks.push(e.data);
  rec.start(100); // small chunks
}

async function stopRec() {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    rec.onstop = async () => {
      stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
      const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: "audio/webm" });
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", blob, "voice.webm");
      fd.append("model", "whisper Grasslands: "speech-to-text" call using whisper-1
      const r = await fetch("/speech/transcribe", { method: "POST", body: fd });
      const out = await r.json();
      resolve(out.text || "");
    };
    rec.stop();
  });
}

const mic = document.getElementById("mic");
mic.addEventListener("mousedown", startRec);
mic.addEventListener("mouseup", async () => {
  const text = await stopRec();
  if (!text) return;
  // Feed into your existing chat pipeline
  await fetch("/chat", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ message: text })
  });
});
</script>
B) Worker route — /speech/transcribe → OpenAI /audio/transcriptions
In Workers you can forward FormData directly (no keys ever in the browser). The platform docs reference transcription through /audio/transcriptions, and whisper-1 is the classic choice. 

// src/index.js (inside fetch)
if (url.pathname === "/speech/transcribe" && request.method === "POST") {
  // Optional: require sovereign session (recommended). Keep this behind your auth wall.
  // if (!isSovereignSession(request, env)) return new Response("DENY", { status: 401 });

  const inbound = await request.formData();
  inbound.set("model", "whisper-1"); // or your chosen transcribe model

  const r = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}` },
    body: inbound
  });

  const j = await r.json();
  // OpenAI typically returns { text: "..."} for transcription flows
  return Response.json({ text: j.text || "" }, { status: r.status });
}

Worker: /realtime/token calls OpenAI Realtime session create → returns client_secret.value

Browser: opens Realtime connection using that ephemeral key, streams mic audio, receives transcript events, then sends final text into /chat and/or routes to /mission/start

This is more moving parts than push-to-talk, but it’s the best “instant feedback” experience.

🛡️ THE DROP-IN PATCH: WORKER-SIDE WHISPER
This logic sits behind your Session Auth to ensure only you can burn OpenAI tokens.
// src/index.js (Inside fetch router)

if (url.pathname === "/speech/transcribe" && request.method === "POST") {
  const session = env.SESSIONS.get(env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY"));
  const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"));

  // 1. SESSION_AUTH_GUARD (Must have active V2 session)
  const statusRes = await session.fetch("https://session/session/status");
  const { hasKey } = await statusRes.json();
  if (!hasKey) return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED:SESSION_EXPIRED", { status: 401 });

  // 2. LEDGER_LOG: VOICE_INGEST
  await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify({ type: "VOICE_INGEST_INIT", ts: Date.now() })
  });

  // 3. WHISPER_RELAY
  const inbound = await request.formData();
  inbound.set("model", "whisper-1");

  const whisperRes = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY} },
    body: inbound
  });

  const j = await whisperRes.json();
  const transcript = j.text || "";

  // 4. LEDGER_LOG: VOICE_TRANSCRIBED
  if (transcript) {
    await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ 
        type: "VOICE_TRANSCRIBED", 
        length: transcript.length, 
        snippet: transcript.substring(0, 20) + "..." 
      })
    });
  }

  return Response.json({ text: transcript }, { status: whisperRes.status });
}

<!doctype html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Phoenix Throne — Voice</title>
  <style>
    :root {
      --bg: #0f0f1a;
      --panel: #141428;
      --purple: #a855f7;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --text: #f3f4f6;
      --muted: #a1a1aa;
      --danger: #ef4444;
    }
    body { margin: 0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, Arial; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); }
    .wrap { max-width: 980px; margin: 24px auto; padding: 0 16px; }
    .card { background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.35); border-radius: 14px; padding: 16px; box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.35); }
    .row { display: flex; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
    .spacer { flex: 1; }
    .title { font-size: 18px; letter-spacing: .3px; margin: 0 0 10px; }
    .hint { margin: 0 0 14px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.4; }
    input, textarea {
      width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.25);
      background: rgba(0,0,0,.25); color: var(--text); padding: 10px 12px; outline: none;
    }
    textarea { min-height: 120px; resize: vertical; }
    button {
      border: 0; border-radius: 12px; padding: 10px 14px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 650;
      background: rgba(168,85,247,.18); color: var(--text); border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.5);
    }
    button:hover { border-color: rgba(245,158,11,.55); }
    .gold { background: rgba(245,158,11,.14); border-color: rgba(245,158,11,.55); }
    .danger { background: rgba(239,68,68,.14); border-color: rgba(239,68,68,.55); }
    .pill { font-size: 12px; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.35); color: var(--muted); }
    .log { font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace; font-size: 12px; white-space: pre-wrap; color: #cbd5e1; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="card">
      <p class="title">Phoenix Throne — Voice Bridge (Whisper Ingest)</p>
      <p class="hint">
        Flow: Record → /speech/transcribe → transcript → (chat or mission).
        Root secrets stay server-side; browser uses a short-lived session key minted by /session/mint.
      </p>

      <div class="row">
        <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 260px;">
          <label class="pill">Monolith Base URL</label>
          <input id="baseUrl" value="https://YOUR_WORKER_DOMAIN" />
        </div>
        <div style="flex: 1; min-width: 260px;">
          <label class="pill">Sovereign Key (typed each session; not stored)</label>
          <input id="sovereignKey" placeholder="x-sovereign-key" />
        </div>
        <div style="width: 160px;">
          <label class="pill">Session</label>
          <button id="btnMint" class="gold" style="width:100%;">Mint</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="height: 12px;"></div>

      <div class="row">
        <button id="btnMic" class="gold">Hold to Talk</button>
        <span id="micState" class="pill">IDLE</span>
        <span class="spacer"></span>
        <button id="btnSendChat">Send to /chat</button>
        <button id="btnSendMission" class="gold">Send to /mission/start</button>
        <button id="btnHalt" class="danger">HALT</button>
      </div>

      <div style="height: 12px;"></div>

      <label class="pill">Transcript</label>
      <textarea id="transcript" placeholder="(voice transcription appears here)"></textarea>

      <div style="height: 12px;"></div>

      <label class="pill">Log</label>
      <div id="log" class="log"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ---------------------------
    // Utilities
    // ---------------------------
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const log = (msg) => { $("log").textContent = `[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${msg}\n` + $("log").textContent; };

    async function sha256Hex(str) {
      const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
      const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    }
    function b64FromBytes(bytes) {
      let bin = "";
      bytes.forEach(b => (bin += String.fromCharCode(b)));
      return btoa(bin);
    }
    function bytesFromB64(b64) {
      const bin = atob(b64);
      const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
      return out;
    }
    async function hmacSha256B64(keyBytes, msg) {
      const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", keyBytes, { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["sign"]);
      const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(msg));
      return b64FromBytes(new Uint8Array(sig));
    }

    // Handshake V2 signer (session-key based)
    async function signPhoenixV2({ method, path, bodyText, sessionKeyBytes }) {
      const ts = String(Date.now());
      const nonceBytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
      const nonce = [...nonceBytes].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
      const bodyHash = await sha256Hex(bodyText || "");
      const canon = `${method.toUpperCase()}\n${path}\n${ts}\n${nonce}\n${bodyHash}`;
      const sig = await hmacSha256B64(sessionKeyBytes, canon);
      return {
        "x-phoenix-ts": ts,
        "x-phoenix-nonce": nonce,
        "x-phoenix-body": bodyHash,
        "x-phoenix-sig": sig
      };
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // Session key (ephemeral)
    // ---------------------------
    let sessionKeyBytes = null;

    async function mintSession() {
      const base = $("baseUrl").value.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      const sovereignKey = $("sovereignKey").value.trim();
      if (!sovereignKey) { log("Mint denied: missing sovereign key."); return; }

      // Server should: validate x-sovereign-key, mint sessionKeyB64 into SESSIONS DO, return it once.
      const res = await fetch(`${base}/session/mint`, { headers: { "x-sovereign-key": sovereignKey } });
      if (!res.ok) { log(`Mint failed: HTTP ${res.status}`); return; }
      const j = await res.json();
      if (!j.sessionKeyB64) { log("Mint failed: no sessionKeyB64 in response."); return; }

      sessionKeyBytes = bytesFromB64(j.sessionKeyB64);
      log("Session minted: sessionKeyBytes loaded (ephemeral).");
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // Voice recording → /speech/transcribe
    // ---------------------------
    let mediaRecorder = null;
    let chunks = [];

    async function startRecording() {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      chunks = [];
      mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream); // default container varies; Worker relays as file.
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (e) => { if (e.data && e.data.size) chunks.push(e.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = async () => {
        stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: mediaRecorder.mimeType || "audio/webm" });
        await transcribeBlob(blob);
      };
      mediaRecorder.start();
      $("micState").textContent = "RECORDING";
      log("Mic: recording started.");
    }

    function stopRecording() {
      if (mediaRecorder && mediaRecorder.state !== "inactive") {
        mediaRecorder.stop();
        $("micState").textContent = "TRANSCRIBING";
        log("Mic: recording stopped, transcribing...");
      }
    }

    async function transcribeBlob(blob) {
      const base = $("baseUrl").value.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      if (!sessionKeyBytes) { log("Transcribe denied: no session (mint first)."); $("micState").textContent="IDLE"; return; }

      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", blob, "speech.webm");
      fd.append("model", "whisper-1"); // server may override; kept for compatibility

      const res = await fetch(`${base}/speech/transcribe`, { method: "POST", body: fd });
      const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      const text = (j && j.text) ? j.text : "";
      $("transcript").value = text;
      $("micState").textContent = "IDLE";
      log(`Transcribed: ${text ? (text.slice(0, 80) + (text.length > 80 ? "…" : "")) : "(empty)"}`);
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // Transcript → /chat or /mission/start
    // ---------------------------
    async function sendToChat() {
      const base = $("baseUrl").value.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      const text = $("transcript").value.trim();
      if (!text) { log("Send to chat: empty transcript."); return; }
      const body = JSON.stringify({ message: text });

      // /chat may be your command-station endpoint; keep it natural.
      const res = await fetch(`${base}/chat`, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body });
      log(`/chat: HTTP ${res.status}`);
    }

    async function sendToMissionStart(actionOverride = null) {
      const base = $("baseUrl").value.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      if (!sessionKeyBytes) { log("Mission denied: no session (mint first)."); return; }

      // Minimal “Rome Protocol” placeholder: you’ll swap this with your real parser.
      // If transcript includes explicit keyword, map it; else send as "CHAT_INTENT".
      const text = $("transcript").value.trim();
      if (!text && !actionOverride) { log("Mission start denied: empty transcript."); return; }

      const action = actionOverride || (
        /(^|\s)(halt|stop)\b/i.test(text) ? "HALT" :
        /(^|\s)(rise|takeoff)\b/i.test(text) ? "TAKEOFF" :
        /(^|\s)(scan)\b/i.test(text) ? "SCAN" :
        "CHAT_INTENT"
      );

      const payload = { action, params: {} };
      if (action === "TAKEOFF") payload.params.alt = 2.5;

      const bodyText = JSON.stringify(payload);
      const headers = await signPhoenixV2({ method: "POST", path: "/mission/start", bodyText, sessionKeyBytes });

      const res = await fetch(`${base}/mission/start`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { ...headers, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: bodyText
      });

      const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      log(`/mission/start: HTTP ${res.status} ${j && j.code ? ("code=" + j.code) : ""}`);
    }

    // ---------------------------
    // Wire UI
    // ---------------------------
    $("btnMint").addEventListener("click", mintSession);

    // Push-to-talk behavior (mouse + touch)
    const micBtn = $("btnMic");
    micBtn.addEventListener("mousedown", startRecording);
    micBtn.addEventListener("mouseup", stopRecording);
    micBtn.addEventListener("mouseleave", stopRecording);
    micBtn.addEventListener("touchstart", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); startRecording(); }, { passive: false });
    micBtn.addEventListener("touchend", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); stopRecording(); }, { passive: false });

    $("btnSendChat").addEventListener("click", sendToChat);
    $("btnSendMission").addEventListener("click", () => sendToMissionStart(null));
    $("btnHalt").addEventListener("click", () => sendToMissionStart("HALT"));

    log("Ready. Mint session → hold-to-talk → transcribe → send.");
  </script>
</body>

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const method = request.method.toUpperCase();

    const sessionId = env.SESSIONS.idFromName("SESSION_PRIMARY");
    const session = env.SESSIONS.get(sessionId);

    const ledgerId = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");
    const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(ledgerId);

    // ─────────────────────────────
    // 1️⃣ SESSION MINT
    // ─────────────────────────────
    if (url.pathname === "/session/mint" && method === "GET") {
      const sovereignKey = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";
      if (!sovereignKey || sovereignKey !== env.SOVEREIGN_KEY) {
        return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED", { status: 401 });
      }

      const mintRes = await session.fetch("https://session/mint", {
        method: "POST"
      });

      return mintRes;
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────
    // 2️⃣ SPEECH TRANSCRIBE (Whisper Relay)
    // ─────────────────────────────
    if (url.pathname === "/speech/transcribe" && method === "POST") {

      const statusRes = await session.fetch("https://session/status");
      const { hasKey } = await statusRes.json();
      if (!hasKey) {
        return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED:SESSION_EXPIRED", { status: 401 });
      }

      await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/append", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          type: "VOICE_INGEST_INIT",
          ts_ms: Date.now()
        })
      });

      const inbound = await request.formData();
      inbound.set("model", "whisper-1");

      const whisperRes = await fetch(
        "https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}`
          },
          body: inbound
        }
      );

      const j = await whisperRes.json();
      const transcript = j.text || "";

      await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/append", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          type: "VOICE_TRANSCRIBED",
          ts_ms: Date.now(),
          length: transcript.length
        })
      });

      return Response.json({ text: transcript });
    }

    // ─────────────────────────────
    // 3️⃣ MISSION START (Handshake V2)
    // ─────────────────────────────
    if (url.pathname === "/mission/start" && method === "POST") {

      const bodyText = await request.text();

      // Extract V2 headers
      const verifyPayload = {
        method: "POST",
        path: "/mission/start",
        ts: request.headers.get("x-phoenix-ts"),
        nonce: request.headers.get("x-phoenix-nonce"),
        bodyHash: request.headers.get("x-phoenix-body"),
        sig: request.headers.get("x-phoenix-sig")
      };

      const verifyRes = await session.fetch("https://session/verify", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(verifyPayload)
      });

      const v = await verifyRes.json();

      if (!v.ok) {
        await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            type: "MISSION_AUTH_DENY",
            code: v.code,
            ts_ms: Date.now()
          })
        });

        return new Response(`UNAUTHORIZED:${v.code}`, { status: 401 });
      }

      const req = JSON.parse(bodyText || "{}");

      const ts_ms = Date.now();
      const payload = {
        ts_ms,
        exp_ms: ts_ms + 20000,
        jti: crypto.randomUUID(),
        action: req.action,
        params: req.params || {}
      };

      const missionToken = await mintMissionTokenV2(
        payload,
        env.MISSION_TOKEN_KEY_B64
      );

      await ledger.fetch("https://ledger/append", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          type: "MISSION_AUTH_ACCEPT",
          action: req.action,
          jti: payload.jti,
          ts_ms
        })
      });

      return Response.json({
        ok: true,
        mission_token: missionToken
      });
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
};

export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/mint") {
      const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const b64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes));

      await this.state.storage.put("sessionKeyB64", b64, {
        expirationTtl: 900
      });

      return Response.json({ sessionKeyB64: b64 });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/status") {
      const key = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");
      return Response.json({ hasKey: !!key });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/verify") {
      const body = await request.json();
      const key = await this.state.storage.get("sessionKeyB64");

      if (!key) return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "NO_SESSION_KEY" });

      const nonceKey = `nonce:${body.nonce}`;
      if (await this.state.storage.get(nonceKey)) {
        return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "NONCE_REPLAY" });
      }

      const valid = await internalHmacVerify(key, body);
      if (!valid) return Response.json({ ok: false, code: "SIG_MISMATCH" });

      await this.state.storage.put(nonceKey, 1, {
        expirationTtl: 300
      });

      return Response.json({ ok: true });
    }
  }
}

            # 4. Path B: Chat (Cognitive)

                requests.post(f"{WORKER_URL}/chat", json={"message": text})

if __name__ == "__main__":

import sounddevice as sd
import numpy as np
import webrtcvad
import requests
import queue
import wave
import io
import time
import json

# --- CONFIG ---
WORKER_URL = "https://your-worker.workers.dev"
HARDWARE_URL = "http://localhost:5000/command"
WAKE_WORD = "obi"
MISSION_VERBS = ["rise", "halt", "scan", "takeoff", "land"]

SAMPLE_RATE = 16000
FRAME_DURATION = 30  # ms
VAD_MODE = 2  # 0-3 (3 = aggressive)

vad = webrtcvad.Vad(VAD_MODE)
audio_queue = queue.Queue()


# --- AUDIO CALLBACK ---
def audio_callback(indata, frames, time_info, status):
    pcm = (indata[:, 0] * 32768).astype(np.int16).tobytes()
    audio_queue.put(pcm)


def is_speech(frame):
    return vad.is_speech(frame, SAMPLE_RATE)


def record_speech_segment(max_seconds=6):
    frames = []
    speech_detected = False
    silence_frames = 0
    max_silence = int(0.8 / (FRAME_DURATION / 1000))

    start_time = time.time()

    while time.time() - start_time < max_seconds:
        frame = audio_queue.get()
        if is_speech(frame):
            speech_detected = True
            silence_frames = 0
            frames.append(frame)
        elif speech_detected:
            silence_frames += 1
            frames.append(frame)
            if silence_frames > max_silence:
                break

    if not speech_detected:
        return None

    return b"".join(frames)


def pcm_to_wav_bytes(pcm_data):
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with wave.open(buffer, "wb") as wf:
        wf.setnchannels(1)
        wf.setsampwidth(2)
        wf.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE)
        wf.writeframes(pcm_data)
    buffer.seek(0)
    return buffer.read()


def transcribe_audio(wav_bytes):
    files = {
        "file": ("speech.wav", wav_bytes, "audio/wav")
    }
    try:
        res = requests.post(f"{WORKER_URL}/speech/transcribe", files=files, timeout=15)
        if res.status_code == 200:
            return res.json().get("text", "").lower()
    except Exception as e:
        print("[OBI] Transcription error:", e)
    return ""


def route_intent(text):
    if WAKE_WORD not in text:
        return

    print(f"[OBI] Wake Word Detected: {text}")

    for verb in MISSION_VERBS:
        if verb in text:
            action = verb.upper()
            print(f"[OBI] Kinetic Intent: {action}")

            try:
                m_res = requests.post(
                    f"{WORKER_URL}/mission/start",
                    json={"action": action},
                    timeout=10
                )
                if m_res.status_code == 200:
                    token = m_res.json().get("mission_token")
                    if token:
                        requests.post(
                            HARDWARE_URL,
                            json={"mission_token": token},
                            timeout=5
                        )
                        print("[OBI] Mission dispatched to hardware.")
                else:
                    print("[OBI] Mission start denied:", m_res.text)
            except Exception as e:
                print("[OBI] Mission dispatch error:", e)
            return

    # If not kinetic → cognitive
    try:
        requests.post(
            f"{WORKER_URL}/chat",
            json={"message": text},
            timeout=10
        )
        print("[OBI] Routed to chat.")
    except Exception as e:
        print("[OBI] Chat route error:", e)


def main():
    print(f"[OBI] Ambient Mode V2 Active. Listening for '{WAKE_WORD}'...")

    with sd.InputStream(
        samplerate=SAMPLE_RATE,
        channels=1,
        blocksize=int(SAMPLE_RATE * FRAME_DURATION / 1000),
        callback=audio_callback
    ):
        while True:
            pcm_segment = record_speech_segment()
            if pcm_segment:
                wav_bytes = pcm_to_wav_bytes(pcm_segment)
                text = transcribe_audio(wav_bytes)
                if text:
                    route_intent(text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

🔹 Add This JS (Drop-In)
<script>
const WORKER_BASE = "https://your-worker.workers.dev";
let mediaRecorder;
let audioChunks = [];
let recording = false;

const micBtn = document.getElementById("micBtn");

async function startRecording() {
  const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
  mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
  audioChunks = [];

  mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = e => audioChunks.push(e.data);

  mediaRecorder.onstop = async () => {
    const blob = new Blob(audioChunks, { type: "audio/webm" });
    await sendToWhisper(blob);
  };

  mediaRecorder.start();
  recording = true;
  micBtn.style.background = "#a855f7";
}

function stopRecording() {
  if (mediaRecorder && recording) {
    mediaRecorder.stop();
    recording = false;
    micBtn.style.background = "#f59e0b";
  }
}

micBtn.addEventListener("mousedown", startRecording);
micBtn.addEventListener("mouseup", stopRecording);
micBtn.addEventListener("touchstart", e => { e.preventDefault(); startRecording(); });
micBtn.addEventListener("touchend", e => { e.preventDefault(); stopRecording(); });

async function sendToWhisper(blob) {
  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append("file", blob);

  const res = await fetch(`${WORKER_BASE}/speech/transcribe`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: fd
  });

  const { text } = await res.json();
  if (!text) return;

  const lower = text.toLowerCase();

  // Wake word check
  if (!lower.includes("obi")) {
    console.log("No wake word.");
    return;
  }

  // Mission verbs
  const verbs = ["rise", "halt", "scan", "takeoff", "land"];
  const verb = verbs.find(v => lower.includes(v));

  if (verb) {
    await triggerMission(verb.toUpperCase());
  } else {
    await sendToChat(text);
  }
}

async function triggerMission(action) {
  const res = await fetch(`${WORKER_BASE}/mission/start`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ action })
  });

  const j = await res.json();
  console.log("Mission response:", j);
}

async function sendToChat(text) {
  await fetch(`${WORKER_BASE}/chat`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ message: text })
  });
}
</script>

-- blocks: append-only chain
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocks (
  height      INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  ts_ms       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  type        TEXT    NOT NULL,
  prev_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,
  hash        TEXT    NOT NULL,
  payload_canon TEXT  NOT NULL,

);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS head (
  k TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  v TEXT NOT NULL
);

2) Canonical JSON (sorted keys, JSON-escaped, UTF-8)
This is the CANONJSON_V1_SORTED_KEYS_UTF8_JSONESCAPE pin.

export function canonJSON(x) {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  const norm = (v) => {
    if (v === null || typeof v !== "object") return v;
    if (seen.has(v)) throw new Error("CANON_CYCLE");
    seen.add(v);

    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(norm);

    const out = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) out[k] = norm(v[k]);
    return out;
  };
  return JSON.stringify(norm(x));
}
3) SHA-256 hex (Workers crypto.subtle)
export async function sha256HexUtf8(s) {
  const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(s));
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}
4) Stage-2 append function (fail-closed)
This takes the Stage-1 object, canonicalizes it, derives the block hash deterministically, writes to D1, advances head.

import { canonJSON } from "./canon.js";
import { sha256HexUtf8 } from "./sha.js";

export async function appendBlockD1(db, { type, payloadObj }) {
  if (!type) throw new Error("MISSING_TYPE");
  if (!payloadObj || typeof payloadObj !== "object") throw new Error("BAD_PAYLOAD");

  // Read head
  const heightRow = await db.prepare("SELECT v FROM head WHERE k='height'").first();
  const hashRow   = await db.prepare("SELECT v FROM head WHERE k='hash'").first();

  const height = Number(heightRow?.v || 0);
  const prevHash = String(hashRow?.v || "GENESIS");
  const nextHeight = height + 1;

  const payloadCanon = canonJSON(payloadObj);
  const ts_ms = Date.now();

  // Deterministic block hash
  // Hash input pinned: `${height}\n${prev}\n${ts}\n${type}\n${payloadCanon}`
  const hashInput = `${nextHeight}\n${prevHash}\n${ts_ms}\n${type}\n${payloadCanon}`;
  const hash = await sha256HexUtf8(hashInput);

  // Append (atomic)
  const batch = [
    db.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO blocks(height, ts_ms, type, prev_hash, hash, payload_canon, payload_len) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
    ).bind(nextHeight, ts_ms, type, prevHash, hash, payloadCanon, payloadCanon.length),
    db.prepare("UPDATE head SET v=? WHERE k='height'").bind(String(nextHeight)),
    db.prepare("UPDATE head SET v=? WHERE k='hash'").bind(hash),
  ];

  const res = await db.batch(batch);

  // Fail-closed sanity check: ensure row exists
  const check = await db.prepare("SELECT hash FROM blocks WHERE height=?").bind(nextHeight).first();
  if (!check?.hash || check.hash !== hash) throw new Error("WRITE_VERIFY_FAIL");

  return { ok: true, height: nextHeight, prev_hash: prevHash, hash, ts_ms, payload_len: payloadCanon.length, res };
}

import { appendBlockD1 } from "./append.js";

function ctEq(a, b) {
  a = String(a || ""); b = String(b || "");
  if (a.length !== b.length || a.length === 0) return false;
  let c = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) c |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return c === 0;
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    if (url.pathname === "/stage2/anchor" && request.method === "POST") {
      const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";
      if (!ctEq(key, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY)) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });

      const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
      if (!body?.payload) return new Response("BAD_REQUEST", { status: 400 });

      const out = await appendBlockD1(env.DB, {
        type: "STAGE_1_GENESIS",
        payloadObj: body.payload
      });

      return Response.json(out);
    }

    return new Response("NOT_FOUND", { status: 404 });
  }
};

"use strict";

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — MEGA MONOLITH (REALITY C)
 * VERSION: v100.5-mega-monolith
 * GOSPEL 444: Purple #a855f7 / Gold #f59e0b / Dark #0f0f1a — NO BLUE
 *
 * AUTH:
 * - Admin: x-sovereign-key
 * - Chat:  x-chat-key
 *
 * KEY FIXES:
 * - Local Greeting Gate => ZERO model calls for greetings (kills Wikipedia Voice)
 * - Fail-Closed everywhere
 * - Internal DO auth token uses SOVEREIGN_KEY-derived hash
 */

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 0) CORE UTILS
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function HALT(code, detail, extra) {
  const e = new Error([HALT:${String(code || "ERR")}] ${detail || ""}.trim());
  e.phx = { code: String(code || "ERR"), detail: detail || "", ...(extra || {}) };
  throw e;
}
function assert(cond, code, detail, extra) { if (!cond) HALT(code, detail, extra); }

const te = new TextEncoder();
const td = new TextDecoder();

function safeStr(x) {
  if (x === null || x === undefined) return "";
  if (typeof x === "string") return x;
  try { return String(x); } catch { return ""; }
}
function nowISO() { return new Date().toISOString(); }

async function sha256Hex(input) {
  const data = (input instanceof Uint8Array) ? input : te.encode(String(input ?? ""));
  const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(hash);
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) out += bytes[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
  return out;
}
async function makeRequestId() {
  const seed = ${Date.now()}|${Math.random()}|${crypto.randomUUID?.() || "uuid"};
  return (await sha256Hex(seed)).slice(0, 16);
}
function makeIncidentId(reqId) { return phx_${reqId}; }

// constant-time-ish compare (safe for short secrets in this context)
function ctEq(a, b) {
  const A = safeStr(a);
  const B = safeStr(b);
  if (A.length === 0 || B.length === 0) return A.length === B.length;
  let diff = A.length ^ B.length;
  const max = Math.max(A.length, B.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < max; i++) diff |= A.charCodeAt(i % A.length) ^ B.charCodeAt(i % B.length);
  return diff === 0;
}

function canonJSON(x) {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  const norm = (v) => {
    if (v === null || v === undefined) return null;
    const t = typeof v;
    if (t === "string" || t === "boolean") return v;
    if (t === "number") {
      assert(Number.isFinite(v), "CANON_NUMBER", "NaN/Infinity rejected");
      return v;
    }
    if (t === "bigint") return v.toString();
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(norm);
    if (t === "object") {
      if (seen.has(v)) HALT("CANON_CYCLE", "Cyclic object rejected");
      seen.add(v);
      const out = {};
      for (const k of Object.keys(v).sort()) out[k] = norm(v[k]);
      return out;
    }
    return String(v);
  };
  return JSON.stringify(norm(x));
}

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 1) GOSPEL 444 ENFORCEMENT (dynamic banned indicators)
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function buildBannedIndicators() {
  const w = "bl" + "ue";
  const h1 = "#000" + "0ff";
  const h2 = "#007" + "bff";
  const h3 = "#3b82" + "f6";
  const rgb = "rgb(0,0,255)";
  return [w, h1, h2, h3, rgb];
}
function enforceBrandStringsOnly(str) {
  const s = safeStr(str).toLowerCase();
  for (const tok of buildBannedIndicators()) {
    if (s.includes(String(tok).toLowerCase())) HALT("G444", "Forbidden color indicator in brand surface");
  }
  return true;
}
function enforcePayloadStrings(payloadObj) {
  const skipKeys = new Set(["quarantine", "gospel444_banned", "gospel444_enforcement"]);
  function walk(node, path) {
    if (node === null || node === undefined) return;
    if (typeof node === "string") {
      const s = node.toLowerCase();
      for (const tok of buildBannedIndicators()) {
        const t = String(tok).toLowerCase();
        if (t.startsWith("#") && s.includes(t)) HALT("G444_PAYLOAD", Forbidden token at ${path});
      }
      return;
    }
    if (typeof node !== "object") return;
    if (Array.isArray(node)) {
      for (let i = 0; i < node.length; i++) walk(node[i], ${path}[${i}]);
      return;
    }
    for (const k of Object.keys(node)) {
      if (skipKeys.has(k)) continue;
      walk(node[k], path ? ${path}.${k} : k);
    }
  }
  walk(payloadObj, "");
  return true;
}

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 2) HTTP / AUTH / LIMITS
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const LIMITS = Object.freeze({ maxBodyBytes: 64 * 1024, chatMax: 4000 });

function getHeader(request, name) { return request.headers.get(name) || ""; }

function corsHeaders(origin = "*") {
  return {
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": origin,
    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key, x-phx-request-id",
    "Access-Control-Allow-Max-Age": "86400",
    "Vary": "Origin",
  };
}

function jsonResponse(obj, status, reqId) {
  const body = JSON.stringify(obj);
  enforceBrandStringsOnly(body);
  return new Response(body, {
    status,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      ...corsHeaders(),
      "x-phx-request-id": reqId
    },
  });
}

async function readJsonWithCap(request, maxBytes) {
  const reader = request.clone().body?.getReader?.();
  if (!reader) HALT("BAD_JSON", "Missing body stream");
  let size = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  while (true) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    size += value.byteLength;
    if (size > maxBytes) HALT("REQ_TOO_LARGE", >${maxBytes});
    chunks.push(value);
  }
  const buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  let off = 0;
  for (const c of chunks) { buf.set(c, off); off += c.byteLength; }
  try { return JSON.parse(td.decode(buf)); } catch { HALT("BAD_JSON", "Invalid JSON"); }
}

function requireSovereign(request, env) {
  const expected = safeStr(env?.SOVEREIGN_KEY);
  assert(expected.length >= 16, "MISCONFIG", "SOVEREIGN_KEY missing/weak");
  if (!ctEq(getHeader(request, "x-sovereign-key"), expected)) HALT("AUTH", "Sovereign auth failed");
}

function requireChatKey(request, env) {
  const expected = safeStr(env?.CHAT_KEY);
  assert(expected.length >= 16, "MISCONFIG", "CHAT_KEY missing/weak");
  if (!ctEq(getHeader(request, "x-chat-key"), expected)) HALT("AUTH_CHAT", "Chat auth failed");
}

// INTERNAL token (Worker -> DO) must include the secret, or anyone can mint it.
async function internalTokenForReq(env, reqId) {
  const key = safeStr(env?.SOVEREIGN_KEY);
  assert(key.length >= 16, "MISCONFIG", "SOVEREIGN_KEY missing/weak");
  return await sha256Hex(${key}::PHX_INTERNAL_V2::${reqId});
}

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 3) GATES & ROUTING
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
const GREET_RE = /^(hi|hello|hey|yo|sup|ok|k|thanks|thx)\b[.!?]*$/i;

function isGreeting(msg) {
  const m = safeStr(msg).trim();
  if (!m) return true;
  if (m.length <= 3) return true;
  return GREET_RE.test(m);
}

function wantsResearch(msg) {
  return /\b(search|research|verify|sources|latest|news|update|link)\b/i.test(String(msg || ""));
}

async function callPerplexity(env, message, enableSearch) {
  const key = safeStr(env?.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY);
  assert(key.length > 0, "CONFIG", "PERPLEXITY_API_KEY missing");
  const body = {
    model: "sonar-pro",
    temperature: 0.4,
    max_tokens: 260,
    messages: [
      {
        role: "system",
        content:
          "You are OBI (Agent-99). Brief, operator-facing. No trivia. No citations unless explicitly requested. " +
          "If greeting/small talk, respond as a short command-station operator.",
      },
      { role: "user", content: message }
    ],
    // HARD GATE: disable search unless explicitly requested
    disable_search: !enableSearch
  };

  const r = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": Bearer ${key} },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });

  const raw = await r.text();
  if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, error: Perplexity ${r.status} };

  let j;
  try { j = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return { ok: false, error: "Bad JSON from Perplexity" }; }

  let text = safeStr(j?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || "");
  if (!enableSearch) text = text.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, "").replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ").trim();

  return { ok: true, response: text };
}

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 4) ADMIN MAGIC CHAT (UI)
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function securityHeadersForHtml() {

    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    "X-Frame-Options": "SAMEORIGIN",

}
