  function card(cls, text){
    const d=document.createElement("div");
    d.className="card"+(cls?(" "+cls):"");
    d.innerText=text;
    log.prepend(d);
  }

  async function go(){
    const m=(msg.value||"").trim();
    const k=(keyBox.value||"").trim();
    if(!k){ card("", "ERROR: missing chat key"); return; }
    if(!m){ return; }

    localStorage.setItem("PHX_CHAT_KEY", k);
    card("user", m);
    msg.value="";

    try{
      const res=await fetch(location.origin+"/chat",{
        method:"POST",
        headers:{ "content-type":"application/json", "x-chat-key": k },
        body: JSON.stringify({ message: m, session_id: "magic-chat" })
      });
      const j=await res.json().catch(()=>({error:"Bad JSON"}));
      if(!res.ok){ card("", "ERROR: "+(j.detail||j.error||("HTTP "+res.status))); return; }
      card("", (j.provider?("["+j.provider+"] "):"") + (j.response||""));
    }catch(e){
      card("", "ERROR: "+e.message);
    }
  }

  send.onclick=go;
  msg.onkeydown=e=>{ if(e.key==="Enter") go(); };
})();
  ;

  const html = <!doctype html><html lang="en"><head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/>
<title>PHOENIX OMEGA</title><style>${css}</style></head>
<body>
<h1>🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA</h1>
<div class="card">
  <div class="row">
    <input id="key" placeholder="x-chat-key (stored locally)" style="flex:1"/>
  </div>
  <div class="row" style="margin-top:8px">
    <input id="msg" placeholder="Command…" style="flex:1"/>
    <button id="send">TRANSMIT</button>
  </div>
  <small>Endpoint: /chat (Chat Key). Admin endpoints require x-sovereign-key via curl.</small>
</div>
<div id="log"></div>
<script>${js}</script>
</body></html>;

  enforceBrandStringsOnly(html);
  return new Response(html, {
    status: 200,
    headers: {
      ...securityHeadersForHtml(),
      "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
      "x-phx-request-id": reqId,
    },
  });
}

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 5) WORKER ROUTER
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    const reqId = await makeRequestId();
    const incident = makeIncidentId(reqId);

    try {
      const url = new URL(request.url);

      if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
        return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: corsHeaders() });
      }

      // PUBLIC
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return jsonResponse({
          ok: true,
          service: "PHOENIX",
          reality: safeStr(env?.REALITY || "C"),
          version: safeStr(env?.PHX_VERSION || "v100.5-mega-monolith"),
          ts: nowISO()
        }, 200, reqId);
      }

      // UI
      if (url.pathname === "/admin/magic-chat") {
        return await handleAdminMagicChat(request, reqId);
      }

      // CHAT
      if (url.pathname === "/chat") {
        assert(request.method === "POST", "METHOD", "POST only");
        requireChatKey(request, env);

        const body = await readJsonWithCap(request, LIMITS.maxBodyBytes);
        const msg = safeStr(body.message).trim();
        assert(msg.length > 0 && msg.length <= LIMITS.chatMax, "SCHEMA", "Bad message");

        // P0: Wikipedia Voice Kill Switch
        if (isGreeting(msg)) {
          return jsonResponse({
            ok: true,
            provider: "OBI (LOCAL)",
            response: "Phoenix online. Awaiting command."
          }, 200, reqId);
        }

        const search = wantsResearch(msg);
        let out = { ok: true, provider: "OBI", response: "" };

        // Stage 3: use Perplexity only as the router brain (optional)
        if (safeStr(env?.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY).length > 0) {
          const r = await callPerplexity(env, msg, search);
          out.provider = search ? "AGENT-99(RESEARCH)" : "AGENT-99(CHAT)";
          out.response = r.ok ? r.response : [SYSTEM ALERT] ${r.error};
        } else {
          out.provider = "SYSTEM";
          out.response = "Perplexity key missing. Add PERPLEXITY_API_KEY to enable Agent-99 routing.";
        }

        return jsonResponse({ ok: true, provider: out.provider, response: out.response }, 200, reqId);
      }

      // LEDGER APPEND
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
        assert(request.method === "POST", "METHOD", "POST only");
        requireSovereign(request, env);

        const body = await readJsonWithCap(request, LIMITS.maxBodyBytes);
        assert(body && typeof body === "object" && body.payload !== undefined, "SCHEMA", "payload required");
        enforcePayloadStrings(body.payload);

        const stub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("ledger"));
        const tok = await internalTokenForReq(env, reqId);

        const res = await stub.fetch("https://do/append", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "x-phx-internal": tok,
            "x-phx-request-id": reqId
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(body)
        });

        return new Response(await res.text(), { status: res.status, headers: corsHeaders() });
      }

      // VERIFYCHAIN
      if (url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
        requireSovereign(request, env);

        const stub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("ledger"));
        const tok = await internalTokenForReq(env, reqId);

        const res = await stub.fetch("https://do/verifychain", {
          headers: { "x-phx-internal": tok, "x-phx-request-id": reqId }
        });

        return new Response(await res.text(), { status: res.status, headers: corsHeaders() });
      }

      return jsonResponse({ ok: false, error: "NOT_FOUND" }, 404, reqId);

    } catch (e) {
      const code = e?.phx?.code || "ERR";
      const status =
        (String(code).startsWith("AUTH")) ? 401 :
        (code === "MISCONFIG") ? 503 :
        (code === "METHOD") ? 405 :
        423;
      return jsonResponse({ ok: false, error: code, incident_id: incident, detail: safeStr(e.message) }, status, reqId);
    }
  }
};

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * 6) DURABLE OBJECTS
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/**
 * PHOENIX LEDGER (DO v2)
 * Append-only, cryptographically chained.
 */
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.env = env;
  }

  async _auth(req) {
    const key = safeStr(this.env.SOVEREIGN_KEY);
    const tok = req.headers.get("x-phx-internal") || "";
    const reqId = req.headers.get("x-phx-request-id") || "";
    const exp = await sha256Hex(${key}::PHX_INTERNAL_V2::${reqId});
    if (!ctEq(tok, exp)) HALT("UNAUTH_INTERNAL", "Internal token mismatch");
  }

  async fetch(req) {
    const url = new URL(req.url);
    const reqId = req.headers.get("x-phx-request-id") || "";

    if (url.pathname === "/append") {
      await this._auth(req);

      return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
        const body = await req.json();
        assert(body && body.payload !== undefined, "SCHEMA", "payload required");

        const h = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) ?? 0;
        const next = h + 1;
        const prev = (await this.state.storage.get(block:${h}:hash)) ?? "GENESIS";

        const hash = await sha256Hex(PHX:ENTRY:${prev}:${next}:${canonJSON(body.payload)});

        const blockData = { h: next, prev, hash, pl: body.payload, ts: nowISO() };
        await this.state.storage.put({
          [block:${next}]: blockData,
          [block:${next}:hash]: hash,
          "height": next
        });

        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, height: next, hash, request_id: reqId }), {
          status: 200,
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
        });
      });
    }

    if (url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
      await this._auth(req);
      const h = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) ?? 0;
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, height: h, stone_sky: true, request_id: reqId }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }
      });
    }

    return new Response("Ledger OK", { status: 200 });
  }
}

export class PhoenixSessionStore {
  constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; }
  async fetch(request) { return new Response("Session OK", { status: 200 }); }
}

export class PhoenixMemoryDO {
  constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; }
  async fetch(request) { return new Response("Memory OK", { status: 200 }); }
}

export class RateLimiter {
  constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; }
  async fetch(request) { return new Response("OK", { status: 200 }); }
}

  -H "content-type: application/json" \

  -H "content-type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" \

<script>
  const base = location.origin;
  document.getElementById('base').textContent = base;

  const keyInput = document.getElementById('key');
  const stored = localStorage.getItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY') || '';
  keyInput.value = stored;

  function setStatus(s, ok=true){
    const el = document.getElementById('status');
    el.textContent = s;
    el.className = ok ? 'ok' : 'bad';
  }

  async function jget(path, auth=false){
    const headers = {};
    if(auth) headers['x-sovereign-key'] = keyInput.value.trim();
    const r = await fetch(base + path, { headers });
    const t = await r.text();
    let body;
    try { body = JSON.parse(t); } catch { body = t; }
    return { ok:r.ok, status:r.status, body };
  }

  async function jpost(path, payload, auth=false){
    const headers = { 'Content-Type':'application/json' };
    if(auth) headers['x-sovereign-key'] = keyInput.value.trim();
    const r = await fetch(base + path, { method:'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(payload || {}) });
    const t = await r.text();
    let body;
    try { body = JSON.parse(t); } catch { body = t; }
    return { ok:r.ok, status:r.status, body };
  }

  async function refreshAll(){
    const h = await jget('/health');
    if(!h.ok) return setStatus('health fail', false);
    setStatus(h.body.status || 'OK', true);

    const v = await jget('/verifychain', true);
    document.getElementById('verify').textContent = JSON.stringify(v.body, null, 2);

    const d = await jget('/drones/status');
    document.getElementById('drones').textContent = JSON.stringify(d.body, null, 2);
  }

  document.getElementById('handshake').onclick = async () => {
    localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', keyInput.value.trim());
    await refreshAll();
    const v = await jget('/verifychain', true);
    // deterministic greeting marker
    const greeting = (v.ok && v.body && v.body.stone_sky) ? "Phoenix online. Awaiting command." : "ACCESS_DENIED";
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = greeting + "\\n\\n" + JSON.stringify(v.body, null, 2);
  };

  document.getElementById('refresh').onclick = refreshAll;

  document.getElementById('send').onclick = async () => {
    localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', keyInput.value.trim());
    const msg = document.getElementById('msg').value.trim();
    if(!msg) return;
    const r = await jpost('/chat', { message: msg, mode: 'chat' }, false);
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = JSON.stringify(r.body, null, 2);
  };

  document.getElementById('spawn3').onclick = async () => {
    const r = await jpost('/drones/spawn', { count: 3 }, false);
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = JSON.stringify(r.body, null, 2);
    await refreshAll();
  };

  document.getElementById('appendTest').onclick = async () => {
    localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', keyInput.value.trim());
    const r = await jpost('/ledger/append', { event: "test_alpha", ts: Date.now() }, true);
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = JSON.stringify(r.body, null, 2);
    await refreshAll();
  };

  refreshAll();
</script>
</body>
</html>;
}

You said:
Agreed. Your four answers are basically correct. Two surgical corrections, then the clean activation sequence you can actually run.

Correction 1: UI route name Your deployed Worker contract (per your own earlier notes) serves the cockpit at:

GET /adminmagic-chat (single path, no /admin/chat)


If you also have /admin/magic-chat or /adminmagic-chat variants floating around in different artifacts, pick one and make it canonical today. The benchmark gate must target the deployed truth, not the spec.

Correction 2: Ledger binding type/name Your Gate D is Durable Object ledger proxy, not D1. Therefore the only “truth” is:

Worker endpoints (/ledgerappend, /verifychain) → assert env.LEDGER → idFromName("ledger") → DO routes (/append, /verifychain)


So wrangler.toml must include:

[[durable_objects.bindings]] name="LEDGER" class_name="<your ledger DO class>"


and your Worker must export that DO class.

If the class is PhoenixLedgerDOv2, bind exactly that. If you bind PhoenixLedger or PhoenixLedgerDO, Gate D can’t pass because the proxy can’t reach the correct handler set.


---

1. Nano-Kernel → Boot OBI (actual mechanics, minimal contract)



Boot = deterministic state validation + lane selection. No model call is allowed until it passes.

A. Kernel preflight (public)

GET /health

must return ok/status = true/ONLINE

must include expected version

must declare mode = FAIL-CLOSED (or equivalent flag)



B. Sovereign anchor (private, fail-closed)

GET /verifychain with x-sovereign-key

must return ok:true

must include stone_sky:true

capture {height, head_hash}



C. Session lane selection

Chat lane is authorized by x-chat-key (UI uses localStorage["PHXCHATKEY"])

Sovereign lane is authorized by x-sovereign-key (ledger operations)

If either lane key is missing for its operation, return 401/403 and stop.


D. Identity injection (runtime constants) At minimum:

SOVEREIGN_PRESENT: boolean (do not leak key)

LEDGER_HEIGHT, HEAD_HASH

MODE: chat | command | supervised | exception

VERSION_LOCK


E. Local greeting gate fires first

If message matches local intercept set → return local response immediately

Must not call any provider

Must still run outbound “no-blue” enforcement


That’s “OBI boot” in reality: preflight + anchor + lane + local gate.


---

2. Agent 97 as Auditor (definition by responsibilities)



Agent-97 is the system’s enforcement layer, not a personality.

Required behaviors:

Fail-closed enforcement: ambiguous / unsigned / mismatched contract → HALT.

Contract verification: routes and bindings match deployed spec (UI path, ledger DO binding).

Ledger integrity verification: recompute or verify chain head consistency; never accept “simulated heights.”

Drift detection: compare version_lock vs deployed /health; mismatch → HALT.

Claims rejection: any “state is VERIFIED” claim is invalid unless it’s backed by /verifychain + observed height change after append.


Agent-97 = checksum + gatekeeper.


---

3. Gospel 444 (actual enforceable rules)



Palette allowlist:

Void: #0f0f1a

Soul: #a855f7

Gold: #f59e0b


Disallowlist (minimum viable):

Any blue token: #0000ff, 0000ff, and any dynamically assembled variants you scan for


Enforcement layers (ordered the way they must execute):

1. UI served with locked CSS root vars (hard-coded)


2. Inbound payload scan (reject before any processing / append)


3. Outbound text scan (reject before response leaves Worker)


4. “No-blue” test must fail before ledger append attempt


5. Any violation returns fail-closed error JSON (not a partial response)



If a blue token makes it into a response body or an appended ledger entry, Gospel 444 is not enforced.


---

4. Final benchmark parameters (binary, ship/no-ship)



Gate A — UI access

GET /adminmagic-chat returns 200 HTML

loads in browser

no hard console errors / blank page


Gate B — Local Greeting Gate

POST /chat with "hello" using correct x-chat-key

response shows provider = LOCAL (or GATELOCAL)

proves “no model call”

target: < 100ms


Gate C — Sovereign auth is fail-closed

GET /verifychain without x-sovereign-key → 401/403

GET /verifychain with correct key → 200 + stone_sky:true


Gate D — Ledger truth (height must move)

POST /ledgerappend with x-sovereign-key succeeds

next GET /verifychain shows:

height increments by exactly +1

head_hash changes



Gate E — No-blue tripwire

any payload containing 0000ff is blocked

must not append (height must not change)

must fail before any DO append call


Performance targets (only after gates pass):

local gate <100ms

chat p95 <2.5s

ledger append p95 <300ms

unauthorized writes = 0

One more surgical note: the “127.0.0.1:8790/portal” question in that pasted text is off-contract for your Worker deployment. For the benchmark, you don’t care about a local dev portal. You care about the deployed Worker route(s) and the DO binding behaving exactly as specified above.Your patch is structurally right: add a /portal operator UI, canonicalize the admin route, and preserve legacy links. Three concrete contract mismatches to fix before you paste it in (otherwise you’ll “pass UI” but fail ledger/chat lanes under real keys).

Mismatch 1: Chat key localStorage + header name must match deployed UI contract
You stated the existing Magic Chat HTML reads:

localStorage.getItem("PHXCHATKEY") → sends x-chat-key


Your portal currently uses PHX_CHAT_KEY / CHAT_KEY. That will silently break “operator feels like it saved keys” while /chat denies.

Fix (portal JS): read/write PHXCHATKEY as the canonical key.

Read order: PHXCHATKEY first (canon), then your legacy fallbacks.

Write: set PHXCHATKEY always (optionally also set your legacy names).


Mismatch 2: Ledger singleton name must match the Worker proxy
Earlier deployed reality: Worker proxy does:

env.LEDGER.idFromName("ledger") (singleton name = "ledger")


Your alias snippet uses idFromName("GLOBAL_OMEGA_V3"). If your DO instance name differs, you’ll fork the ledger into a second chain and “height increments” won’t be the chain you verify.

Fix: pick ONE singleton name and use it everywhere. If the proxy already uses "ledger", keep "ledger".

Mismatch 3: Ledger route you call from portal must match the Worker router
You said the router already wires both:

/ledger/append

/verifychain

and also legacy /ledgerappend in some variants


Your portal calls /ledger/append, good. But your earlier benchmark/proofs used /ledgerappend. Decide the canonical benchmark endpoint today and alias the other.

Recommended canonical today (clean, REST-ish):

POST /ledger/append

Minimal patch adjustments (surgical)

A) Portal JS: canonical key names

Change the portal JS key init to:

Sovereign: SOVEREIGN_KEY (and/or PHX_SOVEREIGN_KEY if you already used it)

Chat: PHXCHATKEY (canon) + optional legacy


sov.value = localStorage.getItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY')
  || localStorage.getItem('PHX_SOVEREIGN_KEY')
  || '';

chat.value = localStorage.getItem('PHXCHATKEY')   // CANON
  || localStorage.getItem('PHX_CHAT_KEY')
  || localStorage.getItem('CHAT_KEY')
  || '';

And on write:

localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', sov.value.trim());
localStorage.setItem('PHX_SOVEREIGN_KEY', sov.value.trim());

localStorage.setItem('PHXCHATKEY', chat.value.trim());     // CANON
localStorage.setItem('PHX_CHAT_KEY', chat.value.trim());   // optional legacy
localStorage.setItem('CHAT_KEY', chat.value.trim());       // optional legacy

B) Canonicalize Admin UI route (recommendation)

Make one canonical path and redirect all others to it:

Canonical: /adminmagic-chat (as you proposed)

GET /adminmagic-chat → serve UI

GET /admin/magic-chat → 301 → /adminmagic-chat

optionally GET /adminmagic-chat/ → 301 → /adminmagic-chat


That matches your “benchmark name” goal while preserving legacy.

C) Ledger aliases (do not DO-fetch inside Worker unless you must)

If your Worker already has handleLedgerAppend(request, env, reqId) for /ledger/append, then alias /ledgerappend by calling the same handler, not by crafting a DO fetch. That keeps enforcement (auth, no-blue, request-id, logging) identical.

Example:

if (url.pathname === "/ledgerappend") {
  // rewrite to canonical handler
  return await handleLedgerAppend(request, env, reqId);
}

If you truly don’t have a handler and only proxy straight to the DO, then proxy—but keep the singleton name consistent with the rest of the system (likely "ledger"):

if (url.pathname === "/ledgerappend") {
  const stub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("ledger"));
  const u = new URL(request.url);
  u.pathname = "/append"; // DO route
  return stub.fetch(new Request(u.toString(), request));
}

D) htmlResponse header merge (avoid “origin:” header leakage)

You earlier flagged a pattern that can accidentally emit an origin: response header if you spread extraHeaders that contain origin. Your htmlResponse() uses ...extraHeaders. If your securityHeadersForHtml() includes origin, strip it before spreading.

One-screen benchmark verifier (5 gates, PASS/FAIL)

This runs the binary gates against any BASE (local dev or deployed). It also prints basic latency per call; you can wrap it in a loop later for p95.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE="${BASE:-http://127.0.0.1:8790}"
SOV="${SOV:-}"
CHAT="${CHAT:-}"

pass(){ echo "PASS: $*"; }
fail(){ echo "FAIL: $*"; exit 1; }

req_time_ms() {
  # prints total time in ms for a curl call
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{time_total}\n" "$@" | awk '{printf("%d\n", $1*1000)}'
}

echo "BASE=$BASE"

# Gate A — UI loads (canonical + legacy redirect)
code="$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/adminmagic-chat")"
[ "$code" = "200" ] || fail "Gate A /adminmagic-chat expected 200 got $code"
pass "Gate A /adminmagic-chat 200"

# optional: legacy redirects
code="$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" -L "$BASE/admin/magic-chat")"
[ "$code" = "200" ] || fail "Gate A legacy /admin/magic-chat -> 200 after redirect got $code"
pass "Gate A legacy redirect ok"

# Gate C — verifychain fail-closed (no key)
code="$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "$BASE/verifychain")"
[ "$code" = "401" ] || [ "$code" = "403" ] || fail "Gate C negative expected 401/403 got $code"
pass "Gate C negative auth $code"

# Gate C — verifychain with key
[ -n "$SOV" ] || fail "SOV is empty"
vj="$(curl -sS -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" "$BASE/verifychain")"
echo "$vj" | grep -q '"ok"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*true' || fail "Gate C positive missing ok:true"
echo "$vj" | grep -q '"stone_sky"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*true' || fail "Gate C positive missing stone_sky:true"
h0="$(echo "$vj" | sed -n 's/.*"height"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\([0-9]\+\).*/\1/p' | head -n1)"
[ -n "$h0" ] || fail "Gate C positive could not parse height"
pass "Gate C positive auth ok (height=$h0)"

# Gate B — local greeting gate (chat lane)
[ -n "$CHAT" ] || fail "CHAT is empty"
cj="$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/chat" \
  -H "content-type: application/json" \
  -H "x-chat-key: $CHAT" \
  -d '{"message":"hello"}')"
echo "$cj" | grep -Eqi '"provider"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"(GATELOCAL|LOCAL|OBI \(LOCAL\))"' || fail "Gate B provider not LOCAL"
pass "Gate B local greeting provider=LOCAL"

# Gate D — append increments height by exactly +1
aj="$(curl -sS -X POST "$BASE/ledger/append" \
  -H "content-type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" \
  -d '{"payload":{"kind":"SMOKE","msg":"test_alpha","ts":123}}')"
vj2="$(curl -sS -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" "$BASE/verifychain")"
h1="$(echo "$vj2" | sed -n 's/.*"height"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\([0-9]\+\).*/\1/p' | head -n1)"
[ -n "$h1" ] || fail "Gate D could not parse post-append height"
[ "$h1" -eq $((h0+1)) ] || fail "Gate D expected height $(($h0+1)) got $h1"
pass "Gate D height incremented ($h0 -> $h1)"

# Gate E — no-blue blocks and does NOT increment height
set +e
code="$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" -X POST "$BASE/ledger/append" \
  -H "content-type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" \
  -d '{"payload":{"kind":"SMOKE","msg":"0000ff","ts":123}}')"
set -e
# any non-2xx is acceptable block; tighten if you have a canonical deny code
[ "$code" != "200" ] && [ "$code" != "201" ] || fail "Gate E expected block, got HTTP $code"
vj3="$(curl -sS -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOV" "$BASE/verifychain")"
h2="$(echo "$vj3" | sed -n 's/.*"height"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\([0-9]\+\).*/\1/p' | head -n1)"
[ "$h2" -eq "$h1" ] || fail "Gate E expected height unchanged $h1 got $h2"
pass "Gate E no-blue blocked and height unchanged ($h1)"

echo "ALL GATES PASS ✅"

/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * PORTAL + CANON ROUTE NORMALIZATION PATCH (REALITY C)
 * - Canon Admin UI: /adminmagic-chat
 * - Legacy Admin:   /admin/magic-chat -> 301 -> /adminmagic-chat
 * - Canon Ledger:   POST /ledger/append
 * - Legacy Ledger:  POST /ledgerappend -> same behavior

 * - Ledger DO singleton name: "ledger"
 * - htmlResponse strips accidental origin header leakage
 * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

function htmlResponse(body, status = 200, reqId = "", extraHeaders = {}) {
  // Gospel 444 outbound enforcement (assumes enforceBrandStringsOnly exists)
  if (typeof enforceBrandStringsOnly === "function") enforceBrandStringsOnly(body);

  // prevent accidental "origin:" response header leakage
  const { origin, Origin, ...safeExtra } = (extraHeaders || {});

  return new Response(body, {
    status,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
      "x-phx-request-id": reqId,
      ...safeExtra,
    },
  });
}

    .row { display:flex; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; }

    await refreshAll();
    const v = await jget('/verifychain', { 'x-sovereign-key': sk });

    const ok = v.ok && v.body && (v.body.stone_sky === true || v.body.stone_sky === "MATCH");
    document.getElementById('out').textContent =
      (ok ? "Phoenix online. Awaiting command." : "ACCESS_DENIED") +
      "\\n\\n" + JSON.stringify(v.body, null, 2);
  };

  document.getElementById('refresh').onclick = refreshAll;

  document.getElementById('send').onclick = async () => {
    const ck = chat.value.trim();
    localStorage.setItem('PHXCHATKEY', ck);

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PORTAL + ROUTE CANON PATCH (REALITY C SAFE / LOW-COUPLING)
// - Portal: GET / or /portal
// - Canon Admin: GET /adminmagic-chat
// - Legacy Admin: /admin/magic-chat -> 301 -> /adminmagic-chat
// - Canon Ledger: POST /ledger/append  (preferred existing route)
// - Legacy Ledger: POST /ledgerappend -> same behavior (prefer handler; fallback DO proxy)
// - Portal uses localStorage.PHXCHATKEY and header x-chat-key
// - Ledger DO singleton name: "ledger"
// - htmlResponse strips origin leakage
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function htmlResponse(body, status = 200, reqId = "", extraHeaders = {}) {
  if (typeof enforceBrandStringsOnly === "function") enforceBrandStringsOnly(body);
  const { origin, Origin, ...safeExtra } = (extraHeaders || {});
  return new Response(body, {
    status,
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
      "x-phx-request-id": reqId,
      ...safeExtra,
    },
  });
}

function portalHTML() {

<head>

    .card { background: var(--card); border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.25); border-radius: 14px; padding: 14px; }

    input, textarea { width:100%; background:#0b0b14; color:var(--fg); border:1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.25); border-radius: 12px; padding:10px; outline:none; }

    button.alt { background: var(--g); }
    button.ghost { background: transparent; color: var(--fg); border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.35); }
    pre { background:#0b0b14; border:1px solid rgba(168,85,247,.25); border-radius:12px; padding:10px; overflow:auto; }

    </div>

<script>
  const base = location.origin;
  document.getElementById('base').textContent = base;

  const sov = document.getElementById('sov');
  const chat = document.getElementById('chat');

  sov.value =
    localStorage.getItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY') ||
    localStorage.getItem('PHX_SOVEREIGN_KEY') ||
    '';

  chat.value =
    localStorage.getItem('PHXCHATKEY') ||
    localStorage.getItem('PHX_CHAT_KEY') ||
    localStorage.getItem('CHAT_KEY') ||
    '';

  function setStatus(s, ok=true){
    const el = document.getElementById('status');
    el.textContent = s;
    el.className = ok ? 'ok' : 'bad';
  }

  async function jget(path, headers){
    const r = await fetch(base + path, { headers: headers || {} });
    const t = await r.text();
    let body; try { body = JSON.parse(t); } catch { body = t; }
    return { ok:r.ok, status:r.status, body };
  }

  async function jpost(path, payload, headers){
    const r = await fetch(base + path, {
      method:'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', ...(headers || {}) },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload || {})
    });
    const t = await r.text();
    let body; try { body = JSON.parse(t); } catch { body = t; }
    return { ok:r.ok, status:r.status, body };
  }

  async function refreshAll(){
    const h = await jget('/health');
    document.getElementById('health').textContent = JSON.stringify(h.body, null, 2);
    if(!h.ok) { setStatus('health fail', false); return; }

    // no precedence bug: choose a string from the response, else ONLINE
    const s = (h.body && (h.body.status || h.body.service)) ? (h.body.status || h.body.service) : 'ONLINE';
    setStatus(s, true);

    const v = await jget('/verifychain', { 'x-sovereign-key': sov.value.trim() });
    document.getElementById('verify').textContent = JSON.stringify(v.body, null, 2);
  }

  document.getElementById('handshake').onclick = async () => {
    const sk = sov.value.trim();
    const ck = chat.value.trim();

    localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', sk);
    localStorage.setItem('PHX_SOVEREIGN_KEY', sk);

    // CANON write
    localStorage.setItem('PHXCHATKEY', ck);
    // optional legacy writes
    localStorage.setItem('PHX_CHAT_KEY', ck);
    localStorage.setItem('CHAT_KEY', ck);

    await refreshAll();
    const v = await jget('/verifychain', { 'x-sovereign-key': sk });
    const ok = v.ok && v.body && (v.body.stone_sky === true || v.body.stone_sky === "MATCH");
    document.getElementById('out').textContent =
      (ok ? "Phoenix online. Awaiting command." : "ACCESS_DENIED") +
      "\\n\\n" + JSON.stringify(v.body, null, 2);
  };

  document.getElementById('refresh').onclick = refreshAll;

  document.getElementById('send').onclick = async () => {
    const ck = chat.value.trim();
    localStorage.setItem('PHXCHATKEY', ck);

    const msg = document.getElementById('msg').value.trim();
    if(!msg) return;

    const r = await jpost('/chat', { message: msg }, { 'x-chat-key': ck });
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = JSON.stringify(r.body, null, 2);
  };

  document.getElementById('appendTest').onclick = async () => {
    const sk = sov.value.trim();
    localStorage.setItem('SOVEREIGN_KEY', sk);

    const r = await jpost('/ledger/append',
      { payload:{ kind:"SMOKE", msg:"test_alpha", ts:Date.now() } },
      { 'x-sovereign-key': sk }
    );
    document.getElementById('out').textContent = JSON.stringify(r.body, null, 2);
    await refreshAll();
  };

  refreshAll();
</script>
</body>
</html>;
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ROUTER SPLICE (SAFE: prefers existing handlers, minimal fallbacks)
// Paste inside fetch(), after url is computed.
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function applyPortalAndAliasRoutes({ request, env, url, reqId }) {
  // PORTAL
  if (request.method === "GET" && (url.pathname === "/" || url.pathname === "/portal")) {
    const sec = (typeof securityHeadersForHtml === "function") ? securityHeadersForHtml() : {};
    return htmlResponse(portalHTML(), 200, reqId, sec);
  }

  // CANON ADMIN UI
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/adminmagic-chat") {
    if (typeof handleAdminMagicChat !== "function") {
      return new Response("MISCONFIG: handleAdminMagicChat missing", { status: 500 });
    }
    return handleAdminMagicChat(request, reqId);
  }

  // Legacy admin alias → redirect
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/admin/magic-chat") {
    return new Response(null, { status: 301, headers: { "Location": "/adminmagic-chat" } });
  }
  if (request.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/adminmagic-chat/") {
    return new Response(null, { status: 301, headers: { "Location": "/adminmagic-chat" } });
  }

  // LEDGER legacy alias: /ledgerappend → /ledger/append
  if (url.pathname === "/ledgerappend") {
    // Prefer existing handler if present
    if (typeof handleLedgerAppend === "function") {
      return handleLedgerAppend(request, env, reqId);
    }

    // Minimal fallback: DO proxy (singleton "ledger"), no extra helpers
    if (!env || !env.LEDGER) return new Response("MISCONFIG: LEDGER missing", { status: 500 });
    const stub = env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("ledger"));

    const u = new URL(request.url);
    u.pathname = "/append"; // DO endpoint
    return stub.fetch(new Request(u.toString(), request));
  }

  return null; // no match; caller continues routing
}

This version avoids the ReferenceError trap (no LIMITS/readJson/internalToken/requireSovereign/corsHeaders usage in the alias fallback) and fixes the portal status precedence bug by selecting an explicit string field.

SAMPLES="${SAMPLES:-0}"   # set >0 to enable
P95_TARGET_MS="${P95_TARGET_MS:-150}"

p95_ms() {
  # expects newline-delimited ints on stdin
  sort -n | awk -v n="$SAMPLES" '
    NR==1{a[NR]=$1; next}
    {a[NR]=$1}
    END{
      if (NR==0) { print ""; exit }
      idx=int((NR*95+99)/100); if (idx<1) idx=1; if (idx>NR) idx=NR;
      print a[idx]
    }'
}

if [ "$SAMPLES" -gt 0 ]; then
  echo "Sampling /chat LOCAL hello for p95 (n=$SAMPLES)..."
  times=""
  for i in $(seq 1 "$SAMPLES"); do
    t0="$(now_ms)"
    curl -sS -X POST "$WORKER_URL/chat" \
      -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
      -H "x-chat-key: $CHAT_KEY" \
      -d '{"message":"hello"}' >/dev/null
    t1="$(now_ms)"
    echo $((t1 - t0))
  done | tee /tmp/phx_lat_ms.txt >/dev/null

  P95="$(cat /tmp/phx_lat_ms.txt | p95_ms)"
  if is_int "$P95" && [ "$P95" -le "$P95_TARGET_MS" ]; then
    pass "p95 LOCAL hello <= ${P95_TARGET_MS}ms (p95=${P95}ms)"
  else
    fail "p95 LOCAL hello too high (p95=${P95}ms, target=${P95_TARGET_MS}ms)"
  fi
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# FILE: test_reality_c.sh
# SUITE: PHOENIX REALITY C — DIAMOND++ VALIDATOR
# CONTRACT:
#   - Admin auth header: x-sovereign-key
#   - Chat  auth header: x-chat-key
#   - Canon admin UI route: /adminmagic-chat
#   - Canon ledger endpoints: /ledger/append, /verifychain
#   - Legacy ledger alias (optional): /ledgerappend
#
# ENV:
#   WORKER_URL     (required)
#   CHAT_KEY       (required)
#   SOVEREIGN_KEY  (required)
#
# OPTIONAL:
#   EXPECTED_VERSION (default: v100.5-DIAMOND)
#   UI_PATH          (default: /adminmagic-chat)
#   LEDGER_APPEND_PATH (default: /ledger/append)
#   VERIFYCHAIN_PATH   (default: /verifychain)
#   CHAT_PATH          (default: /chat)
#
#   LATENCY_MAX_MS  (default: 150)   # single-shot local gate budget
#   SAMPLES         (default: 0)     # set >0 to compute p95 for LOCAL hello
#   P95_TARGET_MS   (default: 150)
#
set -euo pipefail

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# 0) PRECHECKS
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "❌ jq missing. Install jq."; exit 2; }

WORKER_URL="${WORKER_URL:-}"
CHAT_KEY="${CHAT_KEY:-}"
SOVEREIGN_KEY="${SOVEREIGN_KEY:-}"

EXPECTED_VERSION="${EXPECTED_VERSION:-v100.5-DIAMOND}"

UI_PATH="${UI_PATH:-/adminmagic-chat}"
LEDGER_APPEND_PATH="${LEDGER_APPEND_PATH:-/ledger/append}"
VERIFYCHAIN_PATH="${VERIFYCHAIN_PATH:-/verifychain}"
CHAT_PATH="${CHAT_PATH:-/chat}"

LATENCY_MAX_MS="${LATENCY_MAX_MS:-150}"
SAMPLES="${SAMPLES:-0}"
P95_TARGET_MS="${P95_TARGET_MS:-150}"

if [[ -z "$WORKER_URL" ]]; then echo "❌ WORKER_URL is required"; exit 2; fi
if [[ -z "$CHAT_KEY" ]]; then echo "❌ CHAT_KEY is required"; exit 2; fi
if [[ -z "$SOVEREIGN_KEY" ]]; then echo "❌ SOVEREIGN_KEY is required"; exit 2; fi

WORKER_URL="${WORKER_URL%/}"

# portable milliseconds
now_ms() {
  if date +%s%3N >/dev/null 2>&1; then date +%s%3N; else echo "$(( $(date +%s) * 1000 ))"; fi
}
is_int() { [[ "${1:-}" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; }

pass() { echo "✅ PASS: $*"; }
fail() { echo "❌ FAIL: $*"; exit 1; }

# Strict JSON getter (fails closed if jq errors)
json_get() {
  local json="$1"
  local filter="$2"
  echo "$json" | jq -er "$filter" 2>/dev/null || return 1
}

http_code() { curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "$@"; }

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# 1) GATES
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
echo "TARGET=$WORKER_URL"
echo "EXPECTED_VERSION=$EXPECTED_VERSION"
echo

# Gate 0 — /health must be JSON, ok true, version lock match
HEALTH_JSON="$(curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/health" || true)"
echo "$HEALTH_JSON" | jq -e . >/dev/null || fail "/health returned non-JSON"
OK="$(json_get "$HEALTH_JSON" '.ok')" || fail "/health missing .ok"
[[ "$OK" == "true" ]] || fail "/health ok != true (got $OK)"

VER="$(json_get "$HEALTH_JSON" '.version')" || fail "/health missing .version"
[[ "$VER" == "$EXPECTED_VERSION" ]] || fail "Version lock mismatch (got '$VER', expected '$EXPECTED_VERSION')"
pass "Gate 0 health ok + version lock"

# Gate A — UI access (canonical)
UI_CODE="$(http_code -i "$WORKER_URL$UI_PATH")"
[[ "$UI_CODE" == "200" ]] || fail "Gate A UI expected 200 at $UI_PATH, got $UI_CODE"
pass "Gate A UI 200 ($UI_PATH)"

# Gate C — verifychain negative must be 401/403
NEG_CODE="$(http_code -i "$WORKER_URL$VERIFYCHAIN_PATH")"
[[ "$NEG_CODE" == "401" || "$NEG_CODE" == "403" ]] || fail "Gate C negative expected 401/403, got $NEG_CODE"
pass "Gate C negative auth ($NEG_CODE)"

# Gate C — verifychain positive, parse height
POS_JSON="$(curl -sS "$WORKER_URL$VERIFYCHAIN_PATH" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY")"
echo "$POS_JSON" | jq -e . >/dev/null || fail "verifychain (pos) returned non-JSON"
POS_OK="$(json_get "$POS_JSON" '.ok')" || fail "verifychain missing .ok"
[[ "$POS_OK" == "true" ]] || fail "verifychain ok != true"
STONE="$(json_get "$POS_JSON" '.stone_sky')" || fail "verifychain missing .stone_sky"
[[ "$STONE" == "true" ]] || fail "stone_sky != true"
HEIGHT_BEFORE="$(json_get "$POS_JSON" '.height')" || fail "verifychain missing .height"
is_int "$HEIGHT_BEFORE" || fail "verifychain height is not int (got '$HEIGHT_BEFORE')"
pass "Gate C verifychain ok (height=$HEIGHT_BEFORE)"

# Gate B — Local Greeting Gate: /chat hello must be LOCAL provider + latency under budget
START="$(now_ms)"
CHAT_JSON="$(curl -sS -X POST "$WORKER_URL$CHAT_PATH" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "x-chat-key: $CHAT_KEY" \
  -d '{"message":"hello","session_id":"realityc_diamondpp"}')"
END="$(now_ms)"
LATENCY="$((END - START))"

echo "$CHAT_JSON" | jq -e . >/dev/null || fail "/chat returned non-JSON"
PROVIDER="$(json_get "$CHAT_JSON" '.provider')" || fail "/chat missing .provider"
# Accept a small set of canonical LOCAL markers
echo "$PROVIDER" | grep -Eq 'LOCAL|GATELOCAL|OBI \(LOCAL\)|OBI \(LOCAL\)' || fail "Gate B provider not LOCAL (got '$PROVIDER')"

if [[ "$LATENCY" -le "$LATENCY_MAX_MS" ]]; then
  pass "Gate B local greeting + latency <= ${LATENCY_MAX_MS}ms (${LATENCY}ms)"
else
  fail "Gate B local latency too high (${LATENCY}ms > ${LATENCY_MAX_MS}ms)"
fi

# Gate D — Ledger append increments height by exactly +1
APP_JSON="$(curl -sS -X POST "$WORKER_URL$LEDGER_APPEND_PATH" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" \
  -d '{"payload":{"kind":"SMOKE","msg":"append-ok","ts":0}}')"
echo "$APP_JSON" | jq -e . >/dev/null || fail "ledger append returned non-JSON"
APP_OK="$(json_get "$APP_JSON" '.ok')" || fail "append missing .ok"
[[ "$APP_OK" == "true" ]] || fail "append ok != true"

VERIFY_AFTER="$(curl -sS "$WORKER_URL$VERIFYCHAIN_PATH" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY")"
HEIGHT_AFTER="$(json_get "$VERIFY_AFTER" '.height')" || fail "post-append verifychain missing .height"
is_int "$HEIGHT_AFTER" || fail "post-append height is not int (got '$HEIGHT_AFTER')"

EXPECTED_H="$((HEIGHT_BEFORE + 1))"
[[ "$HEIGHT_AFTER" -eq "$EXPECTED_H" ]] || fail "Gate D height did not increment by +1 (before=$HEIGHT_BEFORE after=$HEIGHT_AFTER)"
pass "Gate D ledger height incremented ($HEIGHT_BEFORE -> $HEIGHT_AFTER)"

# Gate E — No-blue tripwire blocks + height must NOT change
set +e
BLUE_CODE="$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" -X POST "$WORKER_URL$LEDGER_APPEND_PATH" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" \
  -d '{"payload":{"kind":"SMOKE","msg":"0000ff","ts":1}}')"
set -e

# Any non-2xx is acceptable here; tighten later if you standardize deny codes.
if [[ "$BLUE_CODE" == "200" || "$BLUE_CODE" == "201" ]]; then
  fail "Gate E expected block for 0000ff payload, got HTTP $BLUE_CODE"
fi

VERIFY_BLUE="$(curl -sS "$WORKER_URL$VERIFYCHAIN_PATH" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY")"
HEIGHT_BLUE="$(json_get "$VERIFY_BLUE" '.height')" || fail "post-blue verifychain missing .height"
is_int "$HEIGHT_BLUE" || fail "post-blue height not int (got '$HEIGHT_BLUE')"
[[ "$HEIGHT_BLUE" -eq "$HEIGHT_AFTER" ]] || fail "Gate E height changed after blocked payload (was=$HEIGHT_AFTER now=$HEIGHT_BLUE)"
pass "Gate E no-blue blocked and height unchanged ($HEIGHT_AFTER)"

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# 2) OPTIONAL p95 SAMPLING (LOCAL hello)
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
p95_from_stream() { sort -n | awk '{
  a[NR]=$1
} END {
  if (NR==0) { print ""; exit }
  idx=int((NR*95+99)/100); if (idx<1) idx=1; if (idx>NR) idx=NR;
  print a[idx]
}'; }

if is_int "$SAMPLES" && [[ "$SAMPLES" -gt 0 ]]; then
  echo
  echo "Sampling LOCAL hello for p95 (n=$SAMPLES)..."
  P95="$(
    for _ in $(seq 1 "$SAMPLES"); do
      t0="$(now_ms)"
      curl -sS -X POST "$WORKER_URL$CHAT_PATH" \
        -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
        -H "x-chat-key: $CHAT_KEY" \
        -d '{"message":"hello","session_id":"realityc_p95"}' >/dev/null
      t1="$(now_ms)"
      echo "$((t1 - t0))"
    done | p95_from_stream
  )"
  is_int "$P95" || fail "p95 computation failed (got '$P95')"
  if [[ "$P95" -le "$P95_TARGET_MS" ]]; then
    pass "p95 LOCAL hello <= ${P95_TARGET_MS}ms (p95=${P95}ms)"
  else
    fail "p95 LOCAL hello too high (p95=${P95}ms > ${P95_TARGET_MS}ms)"
  fi
fi

echo
echo "ALL GATES PASS ✅"
And here is the CI wrapper workflow (copy to .github/workflows/reality-c.yml):

name: Reality C Diamond Validator

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      worker_url:
        description: "Worker base URL"
        required: false
        default: "https://phoenix-rising-protocol.mrmichaelhobbs1234.workers.dev"
      samples:
        description: "Enable p95 sampling (0 disables)"
        required: false
        default: "0"
      p95_target_ms:
        description: "p95 target ms (only if samples>0)"
        required: false
        default: "150"
      expected_version:
        description: "Version lock string"
        required: false
        default: "v100.5-DIAMOND"
  push:
    branches: ["main"]

jobs:
  validate:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install jq
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y jq

      - name: Make script executable
        run: chmod +x ./test_reality_c.sh

      - name: Run Reality C gates
        env:
          WORKER_URL: ${{ github.event.inputs.worker_url || secrets.WORKER_URL }}
          CHAT_KEY: ${{ secrets.CHAT_KEY }}
          SOVEREIGN_KEY: ${{ secrets.SOVEREIGN_KEY }}
          SAMPLES: ${{ github.event.inputs.samples || '0' }}
          P95_TARGET_MS: ${{ github.event.inputs.p95_target_ms || '150' }}
          EXPECTED_VERSION: ${{ github.event.inputs.expected_version || 'v100.5-DIAMOND' }}
        run: |
          if [ -z "${WORKER_URL:-}" ]; then
            echo "❌ WORKER_URL missing. Set repo secret WORKER_URL or pass workflow_dispatch input."
            exit 2
          fi
          ./test_reality_c.sh
Repo secrets required:

CHAT_KEY

SOVEREIGN_KEY

WORKER_URL (optional if you always pass workflow_dispatch input)

If you want p95 on by default in CI, set the workflow default input samples: "50" and keep p95_target_ms: "150" (or whatever target you’re using).
